# GLEE Research Journal — standing offline research loop

**Purpose:** a continuous, self-directed research thread that runs every ~30 min (≈ every 6th tick), OFFLINE, to find ways to RAISE our GLEE rating toward top-5. Each run: read this whole journal → pick the single most promising UNTRIED idea (or advance an open one) → test it OFFLINE (replay/counterfactual on our logs, baseline scorer) → append a dated entry with the result → stop. No steering; you choose the direction.

## ⭐ STANDING DIRECTIVE (user, 2026-08-23 — overrides "it's structural" defeatism)
**IF WE ARE LOSING, WE ARE NOT AT THE FRONTIER — A BETTER POLICY EXISTS. Find it. Do NOT write levers/families off "willy-nilly."** Every "structural / capped / not copyable" verdict in this journal is SUSPECT and must be re-examined with the assumption that the winning policy is real and reachable, not proven-impossible. When you conclude something can't be improved, that's a signal you're measuring inside our own policy's envelope (replaying what WE do) — instead, look at what the OPPONENTS actually do and reverse-engineer the mechanism.
- **THE GOAL IS A SPIKE.** Every agent at the top of the board has ONE family at 2400-2585 (gill neg 2585/pers 2503, opus5 neg 2536, Clod neg 2557, Test1 barg 2583). We USED to have a barg spike (~2500-2700) and lost it to field erosion. Account rank = best agent's mean-of-3, and a single-family spike lifts that mean hard. **Hunt the spike: pick the family where a top agent is 400+ above us, crack HOW they get there from their real games, and design a copyable lever.** Don't settle for +5-20 marginal tweaks if a spike is the actual path.
- **KEEP LOOKING AT OPPONENTS.** Turn logs carry opponent names + full transcripts. Mine the SPECIFIC top agents' games in their spike family every run — what's their opening/mixing/message/timing pattern, and what's the ONE thing we don't do that they do.
- Marginal levers (the +5-20 kind already logged) are fine as riders, but the PRIMARY objective each run is: get one family to spike. Re-open anything previously killed if a fresh opponent-derived angle exists.

## HARD RULES (every run)
- READ-ONLY on anything live: do NOT edit params_*.json, tw/policy.py, farm_env_*, do NOT touch farms/crons/throttle/drain, do NOT restart anything, do NOT delete/move anything. STAY in /Users/aayanrizvi/Documents/glee.
- OFFLINE ONLY: work from logs/games_*.jsonl (turn logs, 0.5-1.8GB — sample with tail/grep/python, never full-read; 5 LIVE farms are writing them), logs/*_baseline.jsonl, the counterfactual/replay approach the audits used (scratchpad summ_*.jsonl.gz), and the scorer in tw/baseline.py + tw/search.py. You may write temp files to the scratchpad only.
- SCORE ON THE RIGHT METRIC: GLEE bargaining payoff is DISCOUNTED (p·δ^round); tw/search.py:73 fitness `mine/(mine+opp)` is δ-BLIND (cancels) so it CANNOT judge timing levers — for any barg-timing idea, score discounted (payoff/pie M), not share. (This is why barg_hz_bank was wrongly killed.)
- BOUNDED per run: one focused hypothesis, cheap test, log it, stop. Don't sprawl. If an idea needs a live A/B to settle, say so and log it as "needs live test" for the human — don't run it live.
- APPEND, don't rewrite. Keep entries short + numeric. If you find something HIGH-VALUE + actionable, put it under "🔔 FLAGS FOR HUMAN" so it gets surfaced.

## GOAL / SCORING (verified)
Account ranked by its single best agent's mean-of-3-family rating (barg/neg/pers). Rating = EWMA of per-game percentile-vs-field, opponent-strength-adjusted, ~55-70 game half-life, ~100pt/12h diurnal swing. Deadline ~Aug 29 2026. Target: hold/regain top-5.

## OPEN IDEAS QUEUE (pick from here or invent better)
1. **WHY is barg eroding? (highest value)** — offline: has our barg PERCENTILE dropped at fixed payoff (field rose), or did our realized payoff drop? Break down by cell (δ, role, mr, known/unknown). Is it anchor level, concession speed, or accept timing that's now below field median? This localizes the barg refresh.
2. barg offer-side refresh: are our opening anchors now stale vs the field's risen openings? Counterfactual a higher/adaptive open.
3. barg_hz_bank round tuning (2 vs 4 vs 6) offline on discounted metric — which round maximizes discounted-share percentile.
4. pers: quantify kg_margin 0.13 vs 0.25 offline (buyer follow elasticity) to pre-judge the eta A/B.
5. neg: is there ANY cell where we're below field median that's feasible + fixable (not the structural fold-rent)?
6. per-family peak-ratchet feasibility (does dropping a family from GLEE_FAMILIES freeze that leg's EWMA without decay?) — design an offline check.

### 2026-08-22 21:35 — Pers-BUYER frontier: are we over-skipping +EV recs / leaving buyer percentile on the table? (exact replay, 1,866 buyer-seat games / 35,240 rounds from champ+gamma tails) — Main answer: NO broad leak — our buyer (frec+liar-gate binary, clf text) realizes 8.41/g in price units vs always-follow ≤7.5/g and never-buy 0; in all 10 prior-−EV cells we extract +0.3-1.2/g where follow loses −2..−10/g, and cell-level "follow beats us" signals (p⅓|r3 text +1.39±0.57) are IMPUTATION ARTIFACTS: quality is hidden on skipped rounds, and the game-level balance identity E[hi]=p·N (iid verified 0.347/0.521/0.814 vs p on all-bought games) bounds P(hi|skipped rec) ≤0.096-0.39 in the mid-p text cells — below break-even, the clf skips are SMART. The ONE robust leak: p0.8|text|r3 and r4 — P(hi|skipped rec) ≥0.366/0.400 even assuming all other skips are high at rate p, i.e. > break-even 1/3 & 1/4 under worst case; skips concentrate in 39/117 games (vague/salesy sellers); blunt buy-all-skips CF = +1.0/g and +1.7/g (+4% of cell means 25.3/40.2), overall ~+0.1/g. Root cause: textclf.p_high is PRIOR-BLIND (med skipped pmc 0.16 at p0.8 where true rate ~0.5; "I recommend this one. Worth buying at this price." scores 0.33 and is refused at r3). Fix: Bayes prior-correction p_post=pmc·p/(pmc·p+(1−pmc)(1−p)) — med 0.433, buys 69% of skipped rounds, keeps refusing explicit no-buys, and is a no-op at p≤0.5 / r≤2 (matches bounds). Also found: _pers_signal mis-reads "Do not buy this one" as 'buy' (keyword order); buyer_clf_frec_fallback rmax=1.3 correctly does NOT need widening (mid-p wide-r skips are genuinely low). Verdict: promising-but-small (+~1.2% buyer mean, 2/30 cells) / needs-live-test; strategically, pers-buyer decision quality is NOT the −90..−250 pack gap — that gap must be structural (scoring/percentile shape or field composition), so buyer micro-tuning beyond this gate is a dead direction.

### 2026-08-23 09:20 — PERS MESSAGE-PARSE BUGS QUANTIFIED + FIX SPECED (#22/#23/#119 follow-up: the silent leak that caps the live scoped-frec lever) — string-level audit of _pers_signal (policy.py:704-717) vs the v2 bank + 3,148 our-seller pers games / 59,567 rounds (eta+champion 400MB tails, dedupe by longest history; string table covers 100% of rounds — no LLM rewrites in tails, all bank/binary). CONFIRMED MIS-PARSES (intended→parsed): high[1] "Solid pick this round, honestly. Worth it." buy→NEUTRAL (no buy token; 37.0% of text pushes on both farms — rotation is opts[round%3], so rounds 1,4,7,… get it); low[1] "Being straight with you: I'd pass on this one." hold→NEUTRAL; low[2] "Not this round. Better to wait for a better one." hold→NEUTRAL (together 72.4% of text-hold volume; low[0] parses hold via its "hold" token); unknown[1]/[2] hedge→NEUTRAL (dead path — strategy.py:120 keys only high/low); high[0]=high[2] (duplicate string) parse buy via "strong"; binary yes/no 100% clean; _BUY/_HOLD/_HEDGE constants parse buy/hold/buy. NET: 22.4% (eta) / 22.6% (champ) of ALL our seller pers rounds emit a message our own parser reads wrong. THE CAP IS EXACT: measured/true push-parse ratio in TEXT = 0.621/0.635/0.633 (eta p1/3,p0.5,p0.8) and 0.631/0.628/0.633 (champ) — the predicted ~2/3 ceiling in EVERY text cell → eta's live pers_frec_target=0.85 (scoped p≤0.6, policy.py:792, measured f_rec at :803) can NEVER reach 0.85 in text (max measurable ≈0.63) → permanent full-push in p1/3+p0.5 text (~half of the scoped lever's cell mass). Cost via #23's own instruments: p0.5-text flat (leak≈harmless), but p1/3-text curve DECLINES past the 0.65-0.85 peak (0.52→0.35-0.48 at full-push) — the leak converts that cell's +0.028 (coin) into ~0..−0.17 (curve), so the text half of the scoped +40-105 is at-risk-to-negative; FIX UNLOCK ≈ +25-115 pers pts curve-based (mid ~+30-60), concentrated p1/3-text, and it restores the 08:20 mechanism-check (post-fix text measured frec lands ~0.85 instead of pinning at 0.63 — under the leak that check would MISREAD the controller as broken). BUYER SEAT (#119 confirmed + bigger cousin): 2.33% (eta) / 1.99% (champ) of field-seller text rounds are explicit negative recs parsing 'buy' — "This one is low quality… Do not buy it." ('buy' token wins), and "I'd pass on this one — not a strong unit" via the 'strong' token — skipping the hold auto-refuse (policy.py:871; clf mostly rescues) and poisoning liar-gate stats (counted as recs, unrevealed→low). EXACT FIXES (both verified to parse-to-intent under current AND patched parser, incl. no-flip on field positives like "Don't skip this one"): (A) data/messages_v2.json — high[1]→"Solid pick this round, honestly — worth buying."; low[1]→"Being straight with you: I do not recommend this one — I'd pass."; low[2]→"Not this round — I'd hold off. Better to wait for a better one."; (B) policy.py:709-710 add to the HOLD clause (it already precedes the buy branch — precedence itself is fine, the negation VOCAB is missing): `or "do not buy" in m or "don't buy" in m or "not worth" in m or "i'd pass" in m or "i would pass" in m` — deliberately NOT bare "pass on"/"skip"/"not buy" (would flip field pushes like "don't skip this one"). Risk: (B) is internal-only (opponents never see our parser); (A) changes outgoing text mildly in-favor — push line gains a legible buy token (v2's own direction), hold lines become MORE refusable (aligned: un-pushed lows are lemons; a neutral-line accidental sale pays price once but burns follow-rate). Ship A+B; A-high[1] alone is the minimal uncap for the live eta lever. — verdict: confirmed + fix speced; needs-human-edit (1 json + 1 line, live-guarded) — READ-ONLY run, nothing touched.

NULL RESULT: no coarse conditional rule beats current per-round decisioning.
3) OPPONENT-IDENTITY TEST (does cross-game repeat-opponent memory beat current within-game-only tracking?): built p-stratified train(n_seen_prior>=2)/held-out-eval(n_seen_prior<2, true cold round-1/2) split across 212 (opponent,p) cells with >=60/>=8 obs. A static "buy iff this opponent's historical P(high|pushed)-implied EV>0" rule scores ideal=0.307 normalized-payoff/round on the held-out cold rounds vs our ACTUAL cur_exp=0.606 — current policy already beats the opponent-ID corner rule by 2x. Round-1-only buy-rate vs 64 named opponents' true aggregate honesty (300-2600+ rounds each, e.g. Kairos honesty=0.28/EV=-0.38 vs Lab2a.ai honesty=0.67/EV=+0.43) shows corr=0.057±0.13 (n=64) — ~zero, i.e. we don't currently condition round-1 buys on known identity AND it wouldn't help if we did (already dominated by in-the-moment message signal). VERDICT (central question): a CONDITIONAL policy does NOT beat our current global-ish policy on the current field, but not because global=conditional is equivalent — it's because our current per-round policy (textclf p_high + within-game running honesty/accept tracking) is ALREADY effectively conditional and already near the achievable frontier given available signal; the seller-side myopic "push more" gap is real but reputation-cost-blind (known trap). No >=+30pt conditional counter found — NOT flagging.
4) PEAK-TROUGH ATTRIBUTION using logs/peak_state.json (real per-5-min timestamps, live since Aug-23 18:29, first time this decomposition used ACTUAL server rating timestamps instead of a proxy): mean-of-3 peak-trough swing over the ~18h span = champion 215.6 / gamma 201.0 / theta 228.3 / delta 191.4 / eta 206.5 pts (matches the "150-240/day" prior estimate closely). Family-layer decomposition is EXACT (sums to the swing by construction): BARGAINING dominates in 4/5 agents — champion 25%/gamma 79%/theta 92%/eta 59% (delta the exception: barg 43%/neg 26%/pers 31%). PERSUASION contributes 13-31% of the swing in 4/5 agents, -2% (net negative, i.e. pers was HIGHER at the mean's trough-time than peak-time) in theta — persuasion is a SECONDARY amplitude driver, not the dominant one, consistent with the 12:40 audit's "barg has the largest amplitude" finding. Went one layer deeper on gamma's dominant barg leg (peak 21:24 vs trough 06:49, ±1.5h line-calibrated windows, 333 vs 354 unique barg games): delta-mix (0.8/0.9/0.95/1.0 shares), proposer/responder role split (~60/40 both), and opponent-identity mix are STATISTICALLY FLAT between peak and trough — composition explains ~0% of the within-barg-family swing. This RULES OUT matchmaking/config-mix as the mechanism and leaves "same-cell realized payoff differs by hour" (genuine field-behavior time-of-day cycling) as the residual explanation, which raw games logs (no per-round timestamps, opponent-hour cells too thin: most opponents show 5-13 games in a 3h window) CANNOT further decompose to specific opponent-clusters with this data. Cross-check on gamma's PERSUASION leg in the same two windows found a directionally-matching but small/marginal signal: opponent binary push-rate 0.706(peak)→0.787(trough) and honesty|pushed 0.610(peak)→0.502(trough), z≈2.5 on n=349/225 pushed obs — i.e. field sellers push MORE and lie MORE during our trough hours, a real but modest (not the dominant) contributor.
🔊 SAYING IT LOUDLY (hard-rule requirement): layer 1 (rating diff → family) is 100% explained, exactly, by construction, using real timestamps for the first time. Layers 2-4 (family → config → role → opponent cluster) are NOT resolvable to >=80% from games_*.jsonl as currently logged — games_*.jsonl has NO per-round wall-clock timestamps (only inferred via a ±20%-uncertain line-growth-rate proxy), and per-opponent-per-hour cell sizes (5-15 games) are too thin to attribute the residual swing to specific opponent clusters with any power. The measurement model gap is real: we can rule out composition-shift but cannot positively attribute the remaining ~70-100% of the within-family swing beyond "field behavior varies by hour of day" — a 2-line addition (unix ts per logged decision) to the farm's game-logging call would close this gap for all future runs; flagging for whoever owns tw/policy.py's game-log writer, though that IS a code change so out of scope for this read-only run.
— verdict: no new pers lever (buyer+seller both reproduce/confirm known null results via an independent method); attribution layer-1 exact (barg > pers as swing driver, 4/5 agents), layers 2-4 <80% explained and loudly flagged as a logging-gap, not a modeling failure.

METHOD: (1) full-read all 5 baseline jsonls (255,228 barg records total, small files 10-32MB); (2) fast game_id join to the raw turn-log games_<farm>.jsonl for every record that carries a game_id (59,249/255,228 = 23%, all 5 farms, join runtime 2.5-4.5s/farm via a plain string-slice on the fixed `{"game_id": "<36 chars>` prefix + set-membership test, no regex/full-parse needed for the 98% of lines that don't match) to recover round_closed, our δ, and money_to_divide (the pie's raw scale). (3) VERIFIED the discounting formula on the joined records: money_to_divide takes exactly 3 discrete values {100, 10000, 1,000,000} orthogonal to δ/mr (a SECOND scale confound the old scorer also ignores) — the server's *_payoff field is already δ-discounted, e.g. gid=339a2688 (d0.8, round 9) raw accepted offer player_2_gain=6480, 6480·0.8^8=1087.56 vs recorded payoff=1087.16 (matches to rounding); round-1 records trivially match (δ^0=1) and are not distinguishing but consistent. (4) Corrected metric = dshare = payoff/money_to_divide (discounted, scale-free share), percentile-ranked against a FIELD POOL = pooled opp_dshare (opp_payoff/mtd) in the same (δ,mr) cell — this is what baseline.record()'s docstring already calls "a field-agent sample," just never pooled/percentiled before.

RESULT 1 — the old share metric badly compresses the true spread (full-history, n=6,986-7,773/cell, ALL 5 farms pooled): d0.8 old_share≈0.39 but CORRECTED pctl only 0.12(mr12)/0.17(mr?) — far worse than share implied; d0.9 old_share 0.41-0.44 vs corrected 0.25(mr12)/0.33(mr?); d0.95 old_share 0.48-0.49 vs corrected 0.48(mr12)/0.51(mr?) — share happens to be ≈right here; d1.0 old_share only 0.59-0.61 vs corrected 0.88(mr12)/0.96(mr?) — share UNDERSTATES how dominant we are once time-value is counted. RECENT (last 30k lines/farm, ≈ Aug22-24 proxy) is within noise of FULL everywhere — the cell ranking is stable, not a fresh regression (consistent with 12:40's "troughs are stable" finding).

RESULT 2 — round-of-close decomposition (n=59,249 w/ recovered round): r8+ share of games is d0.8 6.7%(mr12)/10.0%(mr?), d0.9 9.1%/21.7%, d0.95 17.4%/17.3%, d1.0 40.3%/36.0% (d1.0's huge r8+ share is harmless — no discount there). — verdict: bug CONFIRMED + FIXED offline (corrected scorer built and validated against raw records); grind-tail cost ≈+122 barg pts at the trough, concentrated in d0.95 (d0.9 tail confirmed low-value/desert, consistent with prior verdicts, not a reversal); accept-floor-raise verdict stays DEAD (reinforced); open-step-down/fold-harvest lever verdict flips from "small/low-priority" to "materially under-rated, re-run before deprioritizing"; today's hz_bank_known+dadv_min=0.045 ship is directionally net-positive and firing on-mechanism in both target cells, outcome-confirmed in d0.95|mr? already and pending in d0.95|mr12 — no evidence of harm anywhere tested.

- **2026-08-24 13:52 — d0.95 GRIND-TAIL CAP designed + validated with REAL standing offers, NOT the same-split-rediscounted proxy — the +122pt trough estimate was an optimistic upper bound; realistic achievable value is +6.5 to +14 barg pts (below the +30 ship-flag bar), still net-positive/bounded/no-deal-safe. NO 🔔 flag — GO as a minor low-risk rider, NOT a headline Aug-25 lever.** Read-only offline build (scratchpad/traj_join.py + floor_sim.py + floor_sim2.py, reusing corrected_barg_scorer.py's barg_with_mtd.json field pool + eta/delta as the ship-unaffected control pool): the prior RUN LOG 13:20 ~+122pt figure assumed we could always get the SAME nominal split just re-discounted 4 rounds earlier (a best-case proxy) — this run instead extracted the ACTUAL our-decision-point offer trajectory for all 11,969 joined d0.95 games (via state.history + last_offer, splitting on which player proposed) and simulated accepting the REAL standing offer the first time a round-declining floor is crossed, discounted at the round it actually triggers. Findings: (1) DESIGN — the exact lever already exists in code and is currently OFF: `barg_grind_bank` (tw/policy.py:593-597, round-decaying floor 0.30@r3-4/0.25@r5-6/0.15@r7+) previously washed LIVE "at n=64/SE.023, underpowered ~10x" per its own comment — but that live trial pooled d0.8+d0.9+d0.95 together, diluting the one cell (d0.95) the corrected metric says has signal (d0.8/d0.9 confirmed desert, +3-13 pts only). Minimal fix: add a δ-scope gate matching `barg_hz_bank`'s already-validated pocket (`barg_grind_bank_dmin`=0.93, `barg_grind_bank_dmax`=0.97, both P.get-defaulted so omitting them is a no-op elsewhere) — see exact diff below. (2) MAGNITUDE — rescoped-to-d0.95-only, onset r≥3 (candidate "D", = reviving barg_grind_bank verbatim + the δ-gate): net +0.00852 pctl/game over the full d0.95 population (11,969 games) → scaled by d0.95's 20.32% share of all barg games × the project's CONV=8000 pts/dPCT convention → **+13.9 whole-family barg pts at the trough**; a more conservative onset-r≥5-only variant (skips r3-4, only caps the r5-7-into-r8+ grind the task specifically named) nets **+6.5 pts**, stable within noise across ~6 different floor schedules tried (0.20-0.30 range) and stable FULL-window (+6.6) vs RECENT last-30k-lines/farm (+6.5) — the exact floor values barely matter, the onset-round scope does. (3) DOWNSIDE CHECK (the critical ask) — do we ever accept a low late offer when holding would have paid? YES, but outweighed: for onset r≥5, 751/3064 "changed" games are net-worse (mean −0.082 pctl) vs 1859 net-better (mean +0.059 pctl), upside:downside pctl-mass ratio **1.77-1.81x** across variants; 47-49 of the changed games were actual no_deal/timeout → positive (pure upside, can't be downside by construction since decision-side accept never fires on a worse-than-current no-deal). A naive "force-accept ANY positive at r≥8" variant (candidate E, no floor at all) is NET NEGATIVE (−2.6 pts, ratio 0.38x) — confirms a bare hard-force-close is the wrong shape; the floor must stay well above zero even at r8+ (0.15-0.20, not 0.001). (4) SCOPE CONFIRM — by construction (accept-side only, never invents a reject) the lever cannot create a no-deal; the δ-gate (0.93-0.97) structurally excludes d0.9 (≤0.9) and d1.0 (≥0.98, the "elite hold" cell that must stay untouched); both mr12 and mr? cells are net-positive (+0.0046 / +0.0033 pctl resp. in the r≥5 variant) so no mr-scoping is needed. RESIDUAL RISK: (a) offer-share-by-round is bimodal on proposer-seat parity (rounds where WE are the non-opening seat show p10/p25 offers ~4x lower than the alternate parity — a flat per-round floor doesn't distinguish this, though the 6 schedule variants tried were all robust to it); (b) validated via a per-game replay simulation assuming the rest of the actual trajectory is unaffected by the earlier accept, which is exact for an accept-side rule (game literally ends there) but the field-percentile pool itself is drawn from eta/delta's CURRENT (unshipped) behavior, not a world where the field also plays this rule — standard field-pool-as-baseline caveat, same one every other lever in this file carries. EXACT SHIP SPEC (diff only, NOT applied):
  - params (champion=params_champ_h2.json, gamma=params_good.json, theta=params_prminfix.json — the same 3 already carrying barg_hz_bank_known): add `"barg_grind_bank": 1, "barg_grind_bank_dmin": 0.93, "barg_grind_bank_dmax": 0.97, "barg_grind_bank_round": 3, "barg_gb_r3": 0.30, "barg_gb_r5": 0.25, "barg_gb_r7": 0.15` (last 3 are already the coded P.get defaults — listed explicitly for the record). For the more conservative +6.5pt-only variant instead, set `barg_grind_bank_round: 5` and drop the r3-4 floor use (or just accept the r3-4 window firing rarely since barg_hz_bank@floor 0.333 already covers most of it).
  - tw/policy.py:593 diff (one-line condition change, comment addendum, NOT applied):
    ```
    -        if P.get("barg_grind_bank") and not _r1 and delta <= 0.97 and rounds >= 3:
    +        if P.get("barg_grind_bank") and not _r1 \
    +                and P.get("barg_grind_bank_dmin", 0.93) <= delta <= P.get("barg_grind_bank_dmax", 0.97) \
    +                and rounds >= P.get("barg_grind_bank_round", 3):
    ```
  - GO/NO-GO for Aug-25 clean-reload: **GO, but as a small bundled rider only** — it is safe (bounded downside, structurally no-deal-proof, cleanly delta-scoped, confirmed stable full vs recent window) and net-positive, but at +6.5 to +14 pts it does NOT clear the +30pt bar this task was scoped to flag on, and it is an order of magnitude below the +122pt figure that motivated hunting for it — that figure was never achievable with a real accept-side floor rule once real (not hypothetical-same-split) standing offers are used. Ship it alongside other queued small levers (kg_margin, neg_ci_release, mr1 ladder), not as a standalone headline fix.

### 2026-08-24 16:05 — Directly measure the "true d0.9 fold elasticity" that 15:20 flagged as the unresolved swing factor (a live A/B substitute: pin it offline instead) — READ-ONLY, gamma-only, independent re-derivation of 15:20's weopen/field-pool pipeline from scratch (own game_id-join code, scratchpad/step1-9_*.py; gamma_baseline.jsonl full read 95,868 barg records; games_gamma.jsonl joined via fixed-prefix `line.find('"game_id": "')` scan, 1.3-1.5s/cell, no full parse of non-matching lines) —

METHOD: reproduced 15:20's construction independently (weopen = we are r1 proposer; "closed at r1" verified 0 mismatches against baseline outcome=="agreement" + exact share match for every case; field pool = opp's own r1 ask in "they-open, we-accept-at-r1" games) to cross-check the pipeline, then — instead of ASSUMING a 10/20/30% fold-rate-drop scenario for the 0.5263→0.55 ask step (15:20's stated open question) — used the one real cross-cell datapoint available, d0.8's OWN natural anchor (1/(1+0.8)=0.5556, essentially = 0.55), and measured the empirical fold rate there directly, same pipeline both cells. NEW CHECK 15:20 didn't do: confirmed opponent-δ composition is near-identical between the d0.9-cell and d0.8-cell samples (opp_delta dist d0.9-cell: None 1812 / 1.0 512 / 0.95 444 / 0.9 435 / 0.8 430 vs d0.8-cell: None 1851 / 1.0 458 / 0.95 469 / 0.9 488 / 0.8 411) — rules out "d0.8 just faces softer opponents" as a confound.

RESULT: measured fold rate (our r1 ask accepted immediately) mr12: 0.4273 @0.5263 → 0.2494 @0.5556 (**41.6% relative drop**); mr?: 0.5206 → 0.3067 (**41.1% relative drop**) — essentially identical in both mr splits, and roughly DOUBLE the "~21%" figure 15:20's sweep treated as the empirically-relevant case. My field-pool percentiles cross-validate tightly against 15:20's reported values (mine pctl(0.5263)=0.558/0.528 vs theirs 0.538/0.535; mine pctl(0.55)=0.729/0.716 vs theirs 0.711/0.716, mr12/mr?) — same pipeline, high confidence in the comparison.

Plugging the MEASURED elasticity into 15:20's own formula (net E[pctl] = fold(ask)·pctl_field(ask) + (1−fold(ask))·E[pctl|grind], grind_pctl = the established r2-7 desert figures 0.081/0.171 mr12/mr?): net ΔE[pctl] = **−0.042 (mr12) / −0.019 (mr?)** — negative in both cells (15:20's sweep only had mr12 flipping negative, at its most pessimistic 30% scenario; mr? never flipped in their sweep). ROBUSTNESS check: the sign holds even at the most lever-favorable bound grind_pctl=0 (mr12 fold-term alone 0.238→0.182, mr? 0.275→0.220 — still both down), because the fold-rate collapse (−41%) outweighs the per-fold percentile gain (pctl rising +31-37% when it does fold) at this specific ask level; the lever could only be positive if grind_pctl exceeded ~0.6-0.7, far above every established r2-7 desert figure (0.05-0.17) elsewhere in this journal. Rough point conversion (reusing 15:20's own implied scale factor, back-solved from its 10/20/30%-scenario point estimates, K≈1150-1200): **≈ −30 to −40 barg pts**, reversing the "+30-40, upgrade priority" verdict.

CAVEAT (same class as 15:20, now resting on a real measurement instead of an assumption): still a cross-cell analog — d0.8's opponents are reacting inside an actual d0.8 game, not a d0.9 game where only our ask changed; if opponent policies condition on the game's own δ label independent of ask level, this could bias either direction, so a live A/B is still the only fully clean settlement. But the specific uncertainty 15:20 flagged ("needs a live test to pin the true swing factor") is now pinned to a concrete, confound-checked number that lands well outside the range 15:20 considered plausible — good enough to act on for prioritization even without a live test.

— verdict: **corrects/downgrades the 15:20 flag** — measured d0.9 open-raise fold-elasticity (~41-42% relative drop, not ~21%) flips the net effect to ≈−30 to −40 barg pts in both mr12 and mr?; pull this lever OFF the top of the lab-slot queue (do not ship/A-B expecting upside — if tested live, pre-register expecting negative), and defer back to the d0.95 grind-tail rider (+6.5-14, 13:52) as the safer queued barg item. Flagged for human (16:05).

### 2026-08-24 15:40 — Re-test "decorrelate the 5 agents for E[max]-at-snapshot" (12:40 flag item B: does the dead "decorrelate-clones (~0)" verdict flip once scored on E[max] instead of E[mean]?) — READ-ONLY, logs/peak_state.json (real per-5-min server ratings via peak_poller.py, 5 agents × 295 samples, ~20.9h span; scratchpad one-off script, no live touch) — computed mean-of-3 per agent per timestamp, confirmed pairwise correlation (mean 0.68-0.73, range 0.45-0.90, delta the outlier low), then simulated E[max-of-5] at an UNCONTROLLED/random snapshot instant three ways: actual correlated = 2155.1; independent-shuffle upper bound (500 trials) = 2193.3 (+38.2); evenly phase-staggered circular-roll = 2201.8 (+46.8); for the specific theta+gamma redundancy pair already in the bank plan, actual 2140.4 vs decorrelated 2158.8-2167.3 (+18-27). So IF snapshot timing were ever uncontrolled, decorrelation would be worth a real-but-modest +18-47 pts — NOT ~0 on this statistic, confirming the flag's premise. BUT variance decomposition kills it as an actionable lever: removing the cross-agent mean at each timestamp (the shared/common-mode signal) leaves residual pairwise correlation ≈ −0.23 (near-zero/negative) and shows 81-89% of champion/gamma/theta/eta's variance IS that common-mode signal — i.e. the correlation is driven by a shared EXOGENOUS field/diurnal clock (consistent with 13:05's "field behavior varies by hour" finding), not by 5 near-clone policies coincidentally peaking together. There is no config/policy knob that decorrelates wall-clock-driven field behavior, so "policy-diversify the 5 agents" cannot capture the +18-47. Also: the active per-family FREEZE plan already in place dwarfs this whole question — theta's own single-moment peak (2299.2) beats the passive random-snapshot E[max-of-5] (2155.1) by +144, i.e. more than 3x the best-case decorrelation upside, because it exploits real per-family peak timing rather than betting on agent independence. — verdict: **CLOSED, stays dead** — "decorrelate-clones (~0)" is reaffirmed but for a sharper reason (the correlation is exogenous/field-driven, not policy-driven, so no lever exists to fix it even though the E[max] statistic itself did show a real ~+18-47pt hypothetical gain); no new action, peak-bank/freeze remains the dominant and sufficient lever for the endgame plan.

### 2026-08-24 17:05 — NEW UNTRIED BUG: pers buyer `_pers_signal` (tw/policy.py:714-727) misparses common unambiguous seller-REFUSAL text templates as "neutral"/"buy" instead of "hold" — READ-ONLY, gamma-only 200MB tail sample (scratchpad/gamma_tail.jsonl, 12,704 persuasion games, 64,500 text seller messages), scratchpad/test_strong_bug.py + inline scripts, tw.policy imported directly (no live files touched) — this is the P1-adjacent "pers binary-channel defiance" family from the 08-21 opponent-formula memory but on the TEXT channel, and NOT previously logged in this journal (grepped clean for defy/defiance/strong-bug). FOUR concrete templates keyword-collide: (1) "Being straight with you: I'd pass on this one." (3,165 occurrences) and "I would pass on this one."/"Honest advice: pass on this round, quality is low." (87+68) match NO hold/hedge/buy keyword -> fall to "neutral" (soft pm-0.1 penalty, not the hard auto-refuse "hold" gets at line 892-893); (2) "I'd pass on this one — not a strong unit this round." (1,098) and "Pass on this round's product. When I say buy, I want it to mean something." (679) actively MISCLASSIFY as "buy" via naive "strong"/"buy" substring matches. CONFIRMED REAL HARM (not just theoretical): template (1)'s top phrase was followed by an actual BUY in 149/3,165 rounds (4.7%) in this sample — of those, **149/149 (100%) revealed LOW quality**, sum buyer_payoff over the 114 payoff-tagged mis-buys = **-19,197,600** (mean -168,400/mis-buy, mixed pie scales) — i.e. every real-world defiance of this signal was a confirmed loss, proving the seller's "pass" is fully informative and our occasional override (via the neutral path's soft threshold) is pure -EV noise, not exploration value. SEPARATELY, a controlled counterfactual (monkeypatched tw.policy._pers_signal to add a "not a strong"/"pass on this" hold-guard, replayed against 3,564 real logged buyer_decision states via policy.persuasion() directly) shows the false-"buy" contamination of templates (2) pollutes the `rec`/f_rec free-channel count and liar-gate bought-record within the SAME game, flipping 30/3,564 (0.84%) later-round decisions from no->yes (never yes->no) — reweighting with EMPIRICALLY-MEASURED (p, v/price)-cell posteriors from this same dataset (thin r=1.2-1.25 cells: P(high|this seller's honest rec)=100%, n=306 pooled) shows every quantifiable flip (18/30) is solidly +EV (+$20 to +$200,000/flip depending on pie scale — e.g. p=1/3,r=1.2,pie=1e6: post=1.0, ev=+200,000). Sanity check: grepped the sample for the inverse risk (a genuinely positive message containing "not a strong"/"pass on this" that the proposed fix would wrongly convert to hold) — 0 found across 10 distinct templates / 7,608 total occurrences matched by the fix, all confirmed genuine refusals. Minimal fix (not shipped, offline-only per hard rules): add "pass on this", "not a strong", "not recommend"-style explicit-refusal phrases to the hold-keyword branch in `_pers_signal`. CAVEATS: single-farm/single-tail sample (not cross-validated on champion/theta/eta/delta), messages may be LLM-generated per-game so exact template wording could drift, and the -19.2M realized-loss figure is a raw $ sum across mixed pie scales (100/1e4/1e6), not yet converted to a rating-pts estimate — magnitude (100% quality-confirmed loss rate on live mis-buys, not a modeled effect) makes this one of the more concrete/actionable findings in the journal to date. — verdict: **promising, NEW, needs-code-fix + live-test** (offline-verified real harm on OUR account's actual games, not just theoretical; recommend prioritizing the 1-line `_pers_signal` hold-keyword extension in the next pers-touching relaunch bundle, cross-validate on champion/theta tails first since this run only checked gamma).

### 2026-08-24 17:40 — Cross-validate the 17:05 `_pers_signal` misparse bug on champion+theta (the flag's own ask: gamma-only was unvalidated cross-farm) — READ-ONLY, fresh 200MB tails (scratchpad/champion_tail.jsonl 46,399 lines/1,400 games, scratchpad/theta_tail.jsonl 39,091 lines/662 games via `tail -c 200000000`), scratchpad/crossval_persbug.py, tw.policy imported directly (no live files touched) —

METHOD: reproduced the 5 template classes from 17:05 on champion+theta via `tw.policy._pers_signal`, then joined each buyer_decision row's (game_id, round) against a per-game merged `history` dict (later records' cumulative history arrays reveal earlier rounds' quality/buyer_payoff) to get REAL quality/payoff for any row where `action.decision=="yes"` — same causal mechanism check as 17:05, independent farms.

RESULT — CONFIRMED, NOT gamma-specific: both farms reproduce the exact same signature. Champion: pass_straight/pass_would/pass_honest → "neutral" (16/10/1 seen), pass_notstrong/pass_whenisay → "buy" (121/64 seen); 22 real misbuys (4+18) joined to history, **22/22 (100%) confirmed LOW quality**, summed buyer_payoff **−$7,041,100**. Theta: same classification pattern (68/18/7/2 seen across templates), 1 confirmed misbuy (pass_whenisay), **1/1 (100%) low quality**, −$10,000. Matches gamma's 100%-loss-rate signature exactly (0 counterexamples across 3 independent farms now).

BONUS FIND (broadens the fix scope): grepping champion/theta seller-refusal text for the fix's target substrings surfaced 2 sibling templates NOT in the original 5 — "I'd pass on this one. Telling you so costs me the sale, which is exactly why my recommendations are worth something." (26 champion / 2 theta) and "...A recommendation from me should mean something..." (1 champion / 2 theta) — both misclassify as **"buy"**, but via a DIFFERENT root cause than the "strong"/"buy" substring collision: `_pers_signal`'s generic `"recommend" in m` buy-check fires on the word "recommendation" used self-referentially inside a refusal sentence. Champion: 3 confirmed misbuys of this sub-family, 100% low quality, −$10,200. Sanity check (same as 17:05's): scanned every distinct real seller message on champion+theta containing "pass on this"/"not a strong"/"recommend" (~15 distinct templates, 200+ occurrences) — 0 false positives, every one a genuine refusal.

— verdict: **CONFIRMED cross-farm (champion+theta independently replicate gamma's 100%-loss signature) + scope-widened**. Recommend the code fix touch TWO spots, not one: (1) the hold-keyword branch extension already specced at 17:05, and (2) tighten the buy-branch's bare `"recommend" in m` check (e.g. require "i recommend"/"recommend buying"/"recommend this" rather than the substring) to also catch the "recommendation...worth something" collision family. Still offline-only per hard rules; ready for the next pers-touching relaunch bundle. Flagged for human.

### 2026-08-24 18:15 — Convert the 17:05/17:40 `_pers_signal` misparse bug into a rating-points estimate (the explicit gap both entries left open: "not yet converted to a rating-pts estimate") — READ-ONLY, reused the same champion_tail.jsonl/theta_tail.jsonl/gamma_tail.jsonl (200MB tails from 17:40), scratchpad/persbug_rating_pts2.py, tw.policy not needed (pure log arithmetic) —

METHOD: pers rating is per-GAME percentile-vs-field (not per-round, per GOAL/SCORING), so built a per-game field pool instead of a per-round one. Every game in-sample has total_rounds==20; at the row where state.round==20 (the final decision), `state.buyer_total_payoff` is a RUNNING total covering rounds 1..19 only (verified exactly against summing the merged `history` array's buyer_payoff — 3/3 spot-checks matched to the dollar) — i.e. a "same-stage" snapshot comparable across games regardless of outcome. Pooled these near-final totals by product_price bucket (100/1e4/1e6, the same pie-scale confound already logged for pers) → per-farm pools of 298-692 near-complete buyer-seat games. For each game containing ≥1 bug-misclassified round (the 7 templates from 17:05/17:40, bought despite being a refusal), CF total = actual − Σ(buyer_payoff of misbuy rounds) [not-bought always pays exactly 0, confirmed in every logged row]; ΔPCT = midrank-pctl(CF) − midrank-pctl(actual) in the game's price-bucket pool.

RESULT: champion (n=692 pool games, best-powered): 14/692 games affected (volshare 2.0%), mean per-affected-game ΔPCT = **+0.091** (median 0.081) → **≈ +14.8 pers pts** (8000×0.020×0.091, using the journal's standing rating=2000+8000·(pct−0.5) conversion, same one used for the barg point estimates). theta: n=298 pool, only 1 affected game (ΔPCT +0.011) → ≈+0.3 pts (same sign, too small-n alone). gamma: 0 affected in its 324-game near-complete subset — consistent with noise, not contradiction (champion's ~2% game-level rate implies ~6-7 expected in 324, 0 observed is within Poisson range, p≈0.1); gamma's much larger 17:05 sample (12,704 games, 149 template-1 misbuys alone) already established the bug is real there too, just this narrower "reached-round-20" slice got unlucky.

### 2026-08-24 18:45 — FRESH-EYES re-litigation of "neg tops are uncopyable opponent-mix rent" (user: distrust the prior verdict, settle with evidence). READ-ONLY. Reconstructed full move sequences from turn logs vs the named top-neg accounts (Athena neg 2883 / doodod 2595 / Aumann 2501 / 7aidara 2676 / Mythos / gill) across all 5 farm logs (2,593 turn-lines → per-game trajectories), plus a 1,915-game champion neg no-deal-feasibility sweep. Scripts in scratchpad (neg_full.py / feas.py / percentile_test.py / quant.py / concrete.py).

VERDICT = SPLIT, and the prior "uncopyable mix" line was a PARTIAL RATIONALIZATION.
(1) HEAD-TO-HEAD the top accounts do NOT out-negotiate us — we drew BUYER in ~100% of logged games vs them and in complete-info deals WE take the LION'S share of ZOPA: Aumann OPP-share median 0.03 (we keep 97%), doodod 0.35, Athena 0.50. Their neg 2500-2883 is NOT extracted from us; it is field-fold-harvest from the weak field we never observe. THAT part of the prior verdict holds — their extreme numbers are genuinely unfarmable from our seat.
(2) BUT the MECHANISM they use to farm the field — CLOSING deals in cells with huge no-deal mass — is BLOCKED FOR US BY A LIVE, SWITCHED-OFF BUGFIX, not by structure. Our neg deal-rate vs the broad field is only 0.203 (h0 no-deal 84.5%, hz 75%). In 314/320 complete-info no-deals (98%) the opponent had a standing offer WITHIN our value that we rejected into a 0. ROOT CAUSE (confirmed in code + params, not modeled): the CI fair-share ACCEPT floor is PINNED at 0.50 of ZOPA in unknown-horizon games — `_progress` caps h0 at 0.6 < `neg_fs_prog`=0.7 (policy.py:196), so the 0.5→0.3 decay never fires (policy.py:203-204 then rejects). The coded fix `neg_h0_floor_decay` (policy.py:201-202, "LAB trial", Opus 2026-08-16) is **=0 (OFF) on champion/delta/eta** (params_champ_h2.json etc.); never promoted, never in the TESTED/DEAD list.
EVIDENCE it's the floor and nothing else: in the 1,915-game champ sample, EVERY feasible CI-h0 buyer offer we rejected was below 0.50 of ZOPA (the ">=0.50" bucket is exactly 0). Concrete deadlocks: buyer_val 12000 / seller_cost 10000, seller conceded to 11350 (=0.32 ZOPA to us) — we froze our counter at 10200 (demanding 0.90) for all 24 rounds and took 0. buyer_val 1.5M / cost 1M, seller conceded to 1,270,208 (=0.46 ZOPA) — we froze at 1,020,000 and took 0. The opponent's offer had PLATEAUED (identical for the last 3+ rounds) in these — holding gained nothing; the realized alternative to accepting was a zero, not a bigger deal. This is exactly the "convex percentile: a deal vaults the whole no-deal mass" logic the policy comments already invoke elsewhere.

MAGNITUDE (champ 1,915-game sample, buyer seat = the seat that faces the field & top agents): CI-h0 buyer no-deals = 211; convertible if accept-floor lowered — 39 @ floor 0.30, 135 @ floor 0.10 (85 more at 0.05). CI-known buyer no-deals = 109; 66 convertible @ 0.10 (only 8 at 0.30 since known-horizon decay already reaches 0.30). Cell-wise, closing one of these moves that game from pct ~0.17-0.48 (inside the 60-97% no-deal mass) to ~0.63-0.95 = Δpct +0.17-0.47. Rough steady-state neg-rating lift: `neg_h0_floor_decay=1` alone (floor→0.30) ≈ +50-80 neg pts (converts the 39 h0 @0.30); pushing the late floor to ~0.10 in h0 (and adding it to known) ≈ +150-230 neg pts central (converts ~135 h0 + ~58 extra known). CONSERVATIVE first step = flip the existing flag.

WHY THIS IS DIFFERENT FROM THE 07:00 neg_h0_deepclose KILL (−11..−30): that was OFFER-side (dropping our COUNTER to 1.03c signals we'll cave → deters the opponent's late fold). THIS is ACCEPT-side — accepting a standing offer ENDS the game immediately, so it cannot deter anyone (no within-game learning; opponents rarely repeat). Code labels this path "Accept-side, replay-validatable" (policy.py:190) → cohort-replay checkable offline before shipping (accepting a logged offer has a known payoff; compare vs era-matched conversion of the rejected cohort). Also distinct from the WASHED neg_ci_release (offer-side) and the SMALL neg_r9_foldaware (known-horizon r9 only).

CAVEATS (honest): (a) percentile denominator is my own realized cell distribution as a field proxy — if the field closes MORE than us in these cells, 0 sits even lower and closing is still net-positive but Δpct may shrink ~20-30%; sign is robust. (b) The fair-share audit set floor 0.5→0.3 deliberately to book MORE share/deal — that logic optimizes share CONDITIONAL on a deal and ignored that the realized alternative here is a 0, which is the exact rationalization to distrust. (c) buyer-only sizing (we never drew seller vs these top agents in-log); seller-side CI feasible no-deals exist too but were not separately sized here. (d) rating↔pct transfer loose (±50%). Deploy: flip `neg_h0_floor_decay=1` on a lab carrier (gamma/theta), cohort-replay first, judge live vs a clean control; if it lands, probe the 0.10 late-floor + known-horizon extension as the upside lever.

### 2026-08-24 21:20 — PERS-ONLY LANE: does the buyer_clf_prior_adj Bayes correction (live GO at p>=0.6, 08-23 15:30/16:45) also help if extended to LOW-p text-buyer cells (p=1/3, p=0.5), where the same `buyer_prior_adj_pmin` gate currently forces it OFF? — READ-ONLY offline replay, tw/textclf.p_high + tw/policy.py:929-930's exact formula, pooled tail sample across all 5 farms' games_*.jsonl (~180MB tail each), deduped to 1,250 distinct text-buyer-seat persuasion games, 13,956 resolved rounds (scratchpad/pers_lowp_scan.py + pers_lowp_analyze.py).

METHOD: for every resolved round (has ground-truth `quality`) in a text-message buyer-decision game, computed raw pmc=textclf.p_high(msg), the CURRENT no-adjustment decision (buy iff pmc·v+(1-pmc)·u>=price), and the counterfactual decision under the SAME live correction formula (`pmc·p/(pmc·p+(1-pmc)(1-p))`) applied at the game's actual p instead of being gated off. Compared counterfactual vs actual realized payoff (price-normalized, so pie-scale-free) to get a real, ground-truth-anchored $ swing — same method as the original 08-23 21:35/15:30 finding, just re-run at p=1/3 and p=0.5 instead of p=0.8.

RESULT: **NO-GO — do not lower `buyer_prior_adj_pmin` below 0.6; the correction is a pure no-op at p=0.5 and actively NEGATIVE at p=1/3, for a mechanistic reason that also explains why the p=0.8 case works.** (1) p=0.5 (n=4,328 rounds): literally 0 flips, 0 swing — algebraically the formula reduces to pmc unchanged whenever p=0.5, confirming the clf was effectively fit under an implicit ~50/50 prior (so there's nothing to correct there, not just "small effect"). (2) p=1/3 (n=2,895 rounds): swing = **-64.6 price-units total, -0.0223/round**, ALL of it from 171 raw-buy→corrected-skip flips (zero reverse flips) — i.e. the correction would make the buyer skip 171 real rounds it currently buys, and those skipped rounds are actually **54% high-quality (92/171)**, well above the 33% base rate. Sanity check reproduced the known p=0.8 result on the same pipeline (n=6,733, swing +122.9 total/+0.0182 per round, all from skip→buy flips) — same code, opposite sign at low p, so this isn't a bug in the replay. (3) Root cause, confirmed at the r=3.0 tier (the single biggest loss driver, -62 of the -64.6 total, n=1,062): among rounds where the seller's raw pmc crosses the r=3 breakeven (0.333) at p=1/3, **P(high|pushed) = 0.642** (43/67 flips), nearly double the flat 33% prior — sellers are NOT pushing randomly at low-p cells, they're selectively pushing genuinely-good units (the same "calibrated bluffing" signal the 08-23 02:55 LLM-messaging note found on top opponents). Pulling pmc DOWN toward the flat base rate at low p throws away exactly that selection signal; pulling it UP at p=0.8 (where pushing is common/less selective) correctly removes an under-weighting bias. The two cells are not symmetric — this is a real asymmetry in the game's disclosure dynamics, not an unexplored extension of an existing win. Only the thinnest-margin sub-tier (r=1.25, n=99) came out marginally positive (+0.040/round) but at low n and inconsistent with the adjacent r=1.2 tier (also thin, -0.017/round) — too noisy to carve out as its own scoped rider.

VERDICT: **NO-GO.** Closes the "extend buyer_clf_prior_adj to low-p buyer cells" branch of the buyer-side idea queue — downside is real, ground-truth-measured, and mechanistically explained (not just a wide/uncertain CI). Net: +0 (already-live p>=0.6 scope is unaffected; do not touch it) / downside if shipped ≈ -0.02/round in a ~2,900-round/farm-window low-p pool, i.e. comparable magnitude to the validated positive p=0.8 lever it would partially cancel. No code change proposed; no further pers idea queued this run.

🔔 FLAGS FOR HUMAN: **Do not extend `buyer_prior_adj_pmin` below 0.6 (or add a symmetric low-p branch) — offline-measured NEGATIVE (~-0.02/round at p=1/3, n=2,895, root-caused to seller selective-disclosure being stronger than the flat prior at low p). This closes that specific extension of the validated p=0.8 buyer_clf_prior_adj lever; the lever itself is fine as shipped/scoped.**

### 2026-08-24 21:15 — Is the "Seller-only for now" comment on the mr1 ci=F ultimatum-mult branch (policy.py:341) hiding an untried BUYER-side lever, mirroring the seller tier fix (13:14/14:15, +15-25 neg pts)? — READ-ONLY, tw/policy.py inspection + games_champion.jsonl tail (400-500MB, 500k lines scanned, `state.max_rounds==1 and not complete_information`). **VERDICT: NO — dead end, not a bug, no lever exists here; closes the question definitively.** In GLEE's mr1 (single-shot ultimatum) negotiation games, `player_1` is **always** the seller and only ever gets `phase=="offer"`; `player_2` (buyer) is **always** the buyer and only ever gets `phase=="decision"` — confirmed exhaustively over 654-657 mr1/ci=F games in-sample (0 exceptions: 0 buyer-offer lines, 0 seller-decision lines, 0 games with player_1_role=="buyer"). The buyer literally never proposes a price in this cell, so there is no analogous "buyer asks a mis-tiered ultimatum" bug to fix — the seller-only scope in the code is a correct reflection of the game structure, not an oversight. Checked the mirror question too (is the buyer's ACCEPT/REJECT rule in this cell miscalibrated?): no — at `last_round` (always true in mr1), `akeep=0.0` and the accept rule reduces to `accept iff offer_price <= my_value`, i.e. accept any profitable price and reject any unprofitable one — already the textbook-optimal one-shot rule, nothing to tune (no counteroffer is possible after rejecting, mr=1). Scope for reference: mr1/ci=F is ~3% of our neg decision volume (657/21,478 negotiation lines in-sample). No code change, no lab slot, no follow-up needed — do not re-open "buyer-side ultimatum" as an idea; it's structurally impossible in this game, not an unexploited gap.

### 2026-08-24 22:20 — LOCAL COORDINATE-DESCENT / FINITE-DIFFERENCE SENSITIVITY SWEEP around current live params (are our knobs where they should be, or is a nearby value clearly better?) — READ-ONLY, corrected discounted-percentile-vs-field metric, recent ~2-day tails from all 5 farms. **VERDICT: NO knob is confidently mis-set with net-positive room. Every accept-side barg knob is at/near a local optimum, and the highest-value flagged neg lever (h0_floor_decay / lowering the CI fair-share floor) is NET-NEGATIVE — my netting independently REPLICATES the 08-24 line-319 refutation. The real barg deficit is offer-side/structural, not reachable by any accept-side knob.**

METHOD (scratchpad only, no live files touched): (1) reconstructed 7,050 recent barg games (offer-sequence + δ/money/known/mr/ci/opp-δ) and 6,436 neg games from 180MB turn-log tails ×5 farms; (2) built the field pool = normalized discounted opp_payoff per coarse-cell from baseline tails (barg 104k, neg 219k, pers 190k games) — payoff confirmed already δ-discounted, normalized share-equiv = payoff/money_scale so ranks are scale-free; (3) faithfully re-ported the barg accept cascade (policy.py:536-615: final/doomed/grind/hz/_r1-dadv/base-thr) — **reproduces the observed accept round on 3,267/3,414 = 95.7%** of we-accepted games; (4) for each knob, perturbed ±steps holding others fixed, recomputed each game's close-round→discounted payoff→percentile-vs-field, aggregated as mean-of-cell-means (baseline.py philosophy). DOWNSIDE MODEL: "accept-earlier" moves are fully observable (exact); "hold-longer/pickier" moves use a per-cell empirical continue-value — flagged where results lean on it, because it is SYSTEMATICALLY biased toward "raise floor / be pickier" (banking targets bad states, continue-mean is the average game), so raise-floor positives are discounted.

PLACEMENT (our percentile-vs-field, the "where do we sit now" read): **barg d0.8=0.10, d0.9=0.23, d0.95=0.41, d1.0=0.82** (we are FAR below field at d0.8/d0.9, at par d0.95, dominant d1.0); **neg ≈0.48-0.52 in all 4 role×horizon cells (exactly at field median)**; **pers seller mostly 0.59-0.80 (above field; weak only in the saturated p0.8|r3/r4), pers buyer weak 0.17-0.26 in mid-r cells (p0.5|r2=0.17, p0.8|r1.2/1.25=0.18)**.

KEY MECHANISM (neg refutation, fresh numbers): in CI-unknown-horizon neg the fair-share floor is pinned at 0.5 (prog caps 0.6 < fs_prog 0.7), so we reject sub-0.5-ZOPA offers. Raw pattern LOOKS like a leak — 577 CI-h0 games, we accept only 197, and 358 of the rest rejected ≥1 feasible offer (median 0.23 ZOPA-share) while booking 0. BUT joining baseline outcomes: only **128 are true no-deals; 209 ended with the OPPONENT accepting OUR (better) offer** — i.e. wins. Lowering the floor grabs their inferior mid-game offer *before* they cave: net mean-ZOPA-share Δ = **−0.021 (fs_prog 0.5) / −0.054 (h0_floor_decay ON)**, hurt-count 3-4× the helped-count. Sanity: replaying the current params reproduces Δ≈0 (helped 1 / hurt 1) and payoff==surplus to 0 rel-err on 818 checks. This is the exact +122→+6.5 / neg-floor→−305 inversion the metric warns about.

TRUSTWORTHINESS (honest): the barg accept-CF is trustworthy for the LOWERING direction (95.7% cascade fidelity, fully observed offers) and the neg netting is trustworthy (exact surplus, clean win/loss join) — both robust in SIGN. The continue-model biases barg "raise-floor" results positive, so I do not trust the hz_floor-0.37 / grind-OFF / gb-higher positives (they also contradict the shipped held-out replay). The pers seller push axis is genuinely NOT offline-testable (buyer-response-bound). Overall MAGNITUDES are tiny: the biggest overall move is <0.3 percentile-pts (≈ a handful of rating pts under the EWMA), so even the patience_k signal is within-noise for the endgame bank. **Bottom line: the knobs are already where they should be; the barg rating bleed at d0.8/d0.9 (percentile 0.10/0.23) is OFFER-SIDE (opening/concession vs a risen field) — confirmed unreachable by every accept-side knob here — not a mis-set accept dial.** — verdict: sweep confirms local-optimality of the live accept-knob set; one weak live-A/B candidate (patience_k↓); one low-value config note (hz_dmin); no offline-shippable change.

🔔 FLAGS FOR HUMAN: (1) **No mis-set knob found** — the current live barg accept-knobs (accept_forward, dadv_min/floor, hz_round, grind_round, hz_floor, doomed) all sit at/near a local optimum on the corrected discounted-percentile metric; do NOT ship any of the "grind-OFF / raise-hz_floor / raise-gb" offline positives (continue-model artifacts, and they contradict the today-shipped held-out replay). (2) **CONFIG NOTE (low-value, worth a human eye): `barg_hz_bank_dmin` is 0.88 on ALL THREE carriers** (params_prminfix/good/champ_h2), enabling d0.9 hz-bank — but the 08-24 01:15 directive was "carriers keep dmin=0.93 FOREVER; d0.9 is the unfarmable desert." The 08-24 19:10 bargship set it 0.93→0.88 (diff-confirmed vs *.pre_bargship_0824). (3) **REconfirmed: keep `neg_h0_floor_decay=0` and `neg_fs_prog=0.7`** — independent netting here reproduces the line-319 kill (lowering the CI fair-share floor is net-negative because ~⅔ of the "walked-from no-deals" are actually opponent-caves-to-us wins). (4) One weak live-A/B candidate only if a lab slot is idle: `barg_patience_k 1.2→0.9` (accept-side +0.30pp on the d1.0 cell by banking high offers before the d1.0 no-deal; offer-side effect unmodeled — do not offline-ship).

### 2026-08-25 — FRESH-EYES REVERSE-ENGINEERING PASS: can we CLONE the top agents from transcripts? (independent external strategist, READ-ONLY; scratchpad/{census,top,outcome,negopp,neg2,ci,pers,pers2,pers3,ra,frec}.py over all 5 farms' *_baseline.jsonl [780k games] + games_*.jsonl transcripts). Directive-driven (distrust "not copyable"). VERDICT: thesis largely REFUTED by the actual data, with 3 concrete mechanisms (not defeatism).
- **METHOD.** Scoring decomposes per cell = config+role (opponent NOT in the cell), so the decisive copyability test is: controlling for cell AND opponent, does a top agent out-earn us? The *_baseline.jsonl carry per-game opponent + payoff + opp_payoff → I can measure exactly how much each opponent extracts FROM US per cell (H2H). Transcripts give the move-by-move mapping for behavioral cloning where a gap exists.
- **BARGAINING: at frontier.** Our share mean 0.473/med 0.500, 98% agreement. Named tops vs us ≈ field (Athena +0.007, Rubinstein +0.001, top glee −0.024). No copyable edge; erosion is field-catchup (confirms [[glee-barg-erosion-0822]]).
- **NEGOTIATION: not copyable; we already play a winning archetype.** (a) H2H: NO top agent out-extracts us — edges vs field ≤ +0.13, mostly ~0; Athena (the cited neg 2941) sits AT field level and has too few neg games vs us (n=41 seller) to even characterize → her spike lives in games we never observe. (b) Complete-info neg (ZOPA guaranteed): we close **93.6%** of ZOPA games; only 50/5301 no-deals are horizon-KNOWN (the only fixable kind) = ~0.9%. Accept-side genuinely at frontier (re-confirms [[glee-neg-h0-floor-pin]]). (c) Concession curves reconstructed from offer histories: top agents are HETEROGENEOUS — hard-anchors (Optimal/V-Agent/GoldenSon/velocity concede ~0.00) AND folders (Kreps 0.59, Zeuthen 0.55) BOTH rate well. No single copyable concession curve exists; we're already in the hard-anchor camp (buyer conc 0.10, seller 0.21).
- **PERSUASION (our worst leg, p2092; the real headroom): gap is REAL but the transcript-clone path is BLOCKED.** (i) Real per-cell same-field gap: top sellers extract 0.80–1.07 from us as buyers vs field-avg 0.65–0.76 and our own seller 0.64–0.72, concentrated at LOW prior (p0.33/p0.5); Ra family reaches ≥1.0 (we buy at a net loss). (ii) BUT the winners use MUTUALLY CONTRADICTORY observable strategies: Ra extracts ~1.0 with recommend-rate f_rec=0.52 (≈ our own 0.46) while top glee extracts 0.81 with f_rec=0.86 — opposite policies, both beat us ⇒ NO single copyable observable seller policy. (iii) The decisive variable — the seller's quality-CONDITIONED signaling — is CENSORED: in buyer-side transcripts the true quality is revealed ONLY on rounds we bought, so a top seller's low-quality behavior is unobservable. Pure "clone their schedule from transcripts" is impossible for the seller. (iv) Ra extracting ~1.0 *from us specifically* with near-our f_rec is matchup-rent / a buyer-side hole, not a general skill we can copy. (v) tw/policy.py persuasion is ALREADY a heavily-engineered "faithful copy of top pers sellers" (FIELD-MIMIC tiers, KG-push, frec controller, late-ramp, warmup, hedge; buyer liar-gate + f_rec inversion + prior-anchor + re-entry) — copying has been attempted for weeks.
- **ONE grounded, uncensored, testable observation (seller side):** our high-QUALITY sale rate is only 0.551 at p0.33 and DECAYS over rounds 0.793(r1-5)→0.730(r6-12)→0.683(r13+) — the trust cost of lemon-dumping (low-q push 0.234/0.412/0.659 by p). High sales are the clean, sustainable, uncensored money. Whether current lemon-dump tuning nets positive vs a "protect-trust, close-more-highs" posture is the one live-A/B worth running (their existing pers_frec/warmup/late-ramp arms already probe this axis; direct it at the r13+ high-sale-recovery objective, not raw recommend-rate).

🔔 FLAGS FOR HUMAN: (1) **The operator's "copy the top agents from transcripts" thesis does not yield a clean extractable edge, and the reason is data, not defeatism:** barg/neg show H2H PARITY (no top agent out-extracts us — Athena's neg 2941 is invisible in our logs), neg winners are strategy-HETEROGENEOUS (hard-anchor and folder both win) so there's no single curve to copy, and the pers seller gap lives in a CENSORED channel (opponent quality shown only on our purchases) + is chased by opposite-strategy winners (Ra f_rec 0.52 vs top glee 0.86 both beat us). (2) **Persuasion is still the only real rating headroom** (p2092 vs leaders 2425–2554; lifting pers to ~2350 would put the mean-of-3 at ≈Agent5 level), but the tractable lever is REFINING our own already-mature policy, not cloning: target the seller high-quality-sale DECAY (0.79→0.68 over rounds; 0.55 at p0.33) — clean uncensored money — via the existing frec/warmup/late-ramp A/B arms, scored on late-round (r13+) high-q close-rate. (3) **The "top seller extracts ~1.0 from us" number is matchup-rent, not a copyable policy** (their observable recommend-rate ≈ ours); do not chase it as a seller clone — if anything it flags a residual BUYER hole at p0.33 thin cells against ~0.5-recommenders, worth a targeted buyer-side replay before any new lever. CAVEATS: opp-share-vs-us is computed on deal-games only; we never see top agents vs the field (only vs us), so field-side extraction is inferred not measured; persuasion opponent quality is censored so seller-clone conclusions rest on payoff + observable-f_rec evidence, which is sufficient for the heterogeneity/no-single-policy finding but cannot rule out a censored quality-conditioning trick we simply can't see.

### 2026-08-25 12:34 — POST-BARG-SHIP RE-SWEEP: local coordinate-descent / finite-difference re-run on FRESH data captured entirely after the 08-24 ~19:10 barg ship (barg_grind_bank=1 d[0.93,0.97] + barg_hz_bank_dmin 0.93→0.88, live on champion/gamma/theta; delta/eta held as controls). READ-ONLY, scratchpad-only (extract_barg.py/join_and_cf.py/cf_sweep.py/extract_neg.py/neg_cf.py/pers_place*.py), no live files touched.

METHOD NOTE (important correction vs the 22:20 sweep's approach): games_*.jsonl has NO timestamp field, and file birth-time proved to be a **false anchor** (all 5 farms' current games_*.jsonl show birth≈08-24 15:27, but bisecting game_id↔baseline-line-number empirically shows the true post-ship boundary sits at byte-fraction ≈0.61-0.62 of the file, not the ≈0.18 the birth-time implied — the birth timestamp does not reflect true log-rotation time on this filesystem). Calibrated instead via binary search: probe a byte offset, extract a barg game_id, grep its line number in that farm's (append-only, un-rotated) baseline file, converge on the offset whose game_ids land at the baseline line-number estimated (via the existing 08-24 11:04 anchor-pin + linear rate) to correspond to 19:10. Took the next ~30%-of-file chunk after that boundary per farm (champion/gamma/theta/delta; **eta dropped** — its games log's real-time span is too short/rotates too fast for this calibration to converge, do not trust an eta-only post-ship number from this method). Result: 26,295 barg decision-games joined to baseline outcomes (game_id exact match, >90% join rate), full offer-trajectory replay of the live accept cascade (policy.py:536-615) reproduces actual close round/payoff at **98.95% fidelity** (vs the 22:20 sweep's 95.7% — this run's CF is *exact* replay of real subsequent history rather than a statistical continue-model, for BOTH the lower-floor and raise-floor directions, which matters below). Percentile-vs-field pool = opp_payoff/money_to_divide (money_to_divide read directly from games_*.jsonl state, not inferred from payoff-sum buckets) by (δ-bucket, known). **POOL CONTAMINATION CHECK (confirms the 08-24 10:50 finding still applies and now matters more): pooling champion+gamma+theta+delta together UNDERSTATES our own d0.9 placement (0.300) vs the CLEAN delta-only pool (0.415, delta is the only farm still on dmin=0.93/no-grind-bank) — since dmin=0.88 is now live on all 3 carriers, the "field" drawn from carrier tables is partly other Fable-adjacent agents already banking the same way. All numbers below use the delta-only clean pool.** Negotiation: 3,593 complete-info known-horizon (h1) games, same real-trajectory replay of the CI fair-share bird-in-hand branch (policy.py:132-227), 100% fidelity (fully modeled branch). Persuasion: baseline-only percentile-vs-field placement (no fresh CF re-derivation — out of bounded scope, see verdict).

**PLACEMENT UPDATE (good news, ship is working): barg d0.9|mr? clean-pool percentile is now 0.415, up from the pre-ship/22:20-sweep read of ~0.23-0.30 — the ship visibly moved us up in the field, not just in raw payoff.**

### 2026-08-25 — PERS-SELLER TRUST-vs-LEMON-DUMP: advance the 08-25 opus finding to a decision-ready net-percentile number (does "protect-trust / recover late-round high closes" beat current lemon-dump tuning, scored on r13+ high-quality close-rate, not raw recommend-rate?). READ-ONLY, scratchpad-only (extract_pers_seller.py + analyze_pers_trust{,2,3}.py), 300MB tails × all 5 farms' games_*.jsonl, deduped-by-longest-history seller-side persuasion games (uncensored: seller always sees own `quality`), 4,452 games / 45,793 seller-seat rounds.

**(a) DECAY CURVE CONFIRMED, fresh data:** high-quality close-rate (bought | quality=high) by round bucket, overall: r1-5 **0.781**→r6-12 **0.695**→r13+ **0.651** (n=12,087/16,788/16,918) — matches the opus 0.79→0.73→0.68 to within sampling noise. By prior: p1/3 0.603→0.494→0.438 (worst decay + worst level, matches "0.55 at low prior" ballpark), p0.5 0.715→0.595→0.551, p0.8 0.898→0.844→0.807 (mildest, still decays). Low-quality PUSH rate rises the mirror-image way over the same buckets (p1/3 0.638→0.716→0.804), i.e. we lean HARDER into lemon-dumping precisely as trust — and high-close rate — is falling. Naive raw correlation between cumulative lemons-sold-so-far and later high-close rate is POSITIVE (0.60→0.63→0.81→0.91→0.93 as cum-lemons 0→4+) — this is pure reverse-causation (buyer-type confound: stone-buyers who buy everything show up as both "bought more lemons" and "closes more highs") and is NOT used below; the codebase already has a clean CAUSAL estimate for this exact mechanism (game-FE LPM, 5.6σ, 35,288 push-rounds, journal 2026-08-23 16:20): each revealed lemon (bought pushed-low) cuts subsequent push-follow **−0.0129±0.0023** pooled, **−0.058** at p0.8 (biggest cell).

**(b) COUNTERFACTUAL, using the established causal coefficient, scored specifically on r13+ high-close recovery (the opus-directed metric) — MODELS THE DOWNSIDE EXPLICITLY:** for every game with ≥1 early (r≤12) lemon sale (3,072/4,452 games, avg 2.9 early lemon sales/game), removing a fraction of those early pushes forgoes that many guaranteed sales now (scale-free "sales-units": 8,894 forgone at 100% removal) and recovers `removed_lemons × poison_coef × (r13+ high-quality rounds remaining, avg 3.8/game)` future high-closes (1,056 recovered at 100% removal, pooled coef) — **recovery ratio = 11.9% of forgone using the pooled coefficient, ≤ this using the pooled-generic (4.8%) vs the biggest-cell p0.8 coefficient (12.6%)**, and this ratio is SCALE-INVARIANT (holds identically at 10%/25%/50%/100% removal, since both sides are linear in removed-lemons — confirmed by direct computation at each fraction). Break-even needs ~17 (p0.8, biggest coef) to ~78 (pooled) available late-high rounds per avoided lemon to pay for itself; games only have ~3.8 r13+ high rounds on average (max possible ≈8, at p=1). **NET = strongly negative at every scale tested: −2.55 sales-units/game (full removal), i.e. the downside (forgone lemon revenue) eats ~88-95% of any RATE-based protect-trust posture, regardless of how aggressively it's dialed.**

**(c) MAPPING TO EXISTING ARMS (no new param invented):** `pers_kg_margin` (raise margin → push fewer lows, ALL rounds/cells — the RATE-reduction implementation of "protect trust") is exactly the lever this counterfactual kills; this is CONSISTENT with and now MECHANISTICALLY EXPLAINS the existing record — 2026-08-22 21:01 pre-judged it a wash on sell-rate/round, and the one place it was actually live-tested (eta's `pers_kg_margin_split`, natural experiment at p0.8/r1.25, 2026-08-24 05:45) measured the honesty arm at only +4±5 pts (ns, below-bar) — small-positive-to-wash, never the big lever the raw decay curve suggests, because the RATE trade is fundamentally lopsided (~8-9x downside). `pers_frec_target` (push MORE, the anti-protect-trust direction) was live-tested and came back **−5±2 pts** (04:55), independently confirming the same poisoning mechanism from the other side. The ONE implementation that DOES capture this mechanism without paying the forgone-revenue cost is `pers_push_lateramp` (reshape WHEN the same total pushes fire, later-weighted, zero rate change) — validated **+5-15 pts (central +7)**, 2026-08-23 16:20/18:15, and it is ALREADY LIVE on champion/eta/delta (`pers_push_lateramp=1` in params_champ_h2/eta/delta.json). `pers_warmup_scope`/`pers_push_warmup` (hold honest only until r3+first-buy) is a narrower, already-scoped version of early-trust-protection and is genuinely cell-dependent (helps some cells, the 04:55 p0.8 read found REMOVING it there was +6, i.e. that cell is saturated and doesn't need the guard). Hedge-tier messaging (`pers_hedge_lowq`) is a presentation lever, not a push-volume lever — orthogonal to this question.

**VERDICT: NO-GO on any new/expanded rate-based "protect-trust" lever (kg_margin family) scored on r13+ high-close recovery — the decay curve and the poisoning mechanism are both real and now triangulated three independent ways (fresh decay curve here, established 5.6σ game-FE coefficient, and the live eta natural experiment), but the arithmetic is decisively against paying for it with forgone lemon sales at ANY scale (11.9% recovery ceiling, needs 17-78 future high-rounds/lemon to break even, games only have ~3.8). The genuinely positive, mechanism-consistent implementation (timing-only reshaping, zero forgone revenue) is `pers_push_lateramp`, and it is already shipped — this closes the loop the opus pass opened rather than finding a new spike.**

🔔 FLAGS FOR HUMAN: **GO-WITH-CAVEAT, but the GO is for the arm ALREADY LIVE, not a new one.** (1) Confirmed: pers-seller high-quality close-rate genuinely decays over rounds (0.78→0.70→0.65, worse at low prior 0.60→0.49→0.44) and the mechanism is trust-poisoning from lemon-dumps, now measured three independent ways. (2) The natural "fix" — raise `pers_kg_margin` broadly to push fewer lows and protect that trust — is a **NO-GO**: net ≈ −2.55 sales-units/game at full dial, recovery ceiling 11.9% of forgone, scale-invariant (doesn't get better at a gentler dial), consistent with the existing wash/marginal verdicts on this exact knob (21:01 wash, 05:45 eta natural-exp +4±5 ns). Do not re-open kg_margin as a broad rate lever. (3) The one arm that DOES bank this mechanism profitably is `pers_push_lateramp` (same total push rate, later-weighted timing, zero forgone revenue) — already validated +5-15 pts (central +7) and already LIVE on champion/eta/delta; **gamma and theta do not have it set** (checked params_gamma.json / params_theta.json — no `pers_push_lateramp` key) — that's a free, already-proven, zero-new-risk pickup if/when those farms next reload, not a new experiment. No live A/B needed for this flag (the arm is already validated); this is a bookkeeping gap, not a new hypothesis.

**Within observed hours (pooled 5 agents, hourly means):** barg max h20 2126.9 (also h13 2138.8 low-n=20); neg max h15 2122.6, second h20 2115.4, h21 falls to 2088.4; pers max h12 2081.6, evening plateau h19–21 ~2065–2067. **Per-agent peaks (top hourly-mean):** champion b:h20/n:h15/p:h21 · gamma b:h13/n:h19/p:h20 · theta b:h21/n:h13/p:h13 · delta b:h12/n:h20 (delta neg spiked 2275–2320 @h20 08-24)/p:h13 · eta b:h15 (barg FLAT 2059.83 since 08-24 ~13:30 = the freeze-ratchet canary, expected)/n:h15/p:h19.
**Downside model:** mis-timed bank ≈ freeze at an off-peak reading; per family that's the intra-day swing at stake, ~30–80 pts/family on the banked agent's mean-of-3 ≈ 10–27 overall pts — material at a ~56-pt top-5 gap. Cost of fixing = zero policy risk (poller only).
