Read-only observation, no changes made.

## 2026-08-25 ~23:30 — RUN LOG: NEW-FIELD BASELINE (fresh scout, prior verdicts NOT trusted)
**Charter:** field changed (frontier LLM entrants near comp date, top-5 bar 2417 mean-of-3, we're #24; old peaks stale). Establish baseline from RECENT games only. READ-ONLY; tails of games_gamma/games_theta (last 3000 lines each = 849 games: 436 barg / 276 neg / 137 pers; 47% opponent-named; turn logs carry no timestamps, "recent" = tail position).
**Ratings now (peak_state latest, [barg,neg,pers]):** champion 2141/1967/2009 · gamma 2156/2036/2026 · theta 2098/2102/2167 · delta 2103/1967/1987 · eta 2060/2009/1900. Best-per-family: barg 2156, neg 2102, pers 2167 → vs 2417 bar: **neg −315 (worst; 3 of 5 accounts ≤2010), barg −261, pers −250**.
**NEGOTIATION (biggest gap):** we accept in only 70/276 tail games (25%); of 206 no-accept, 51 end on our own offer (opponent-accept invisible — true no-deal rate is between 56-75%), 154 end with US rejecting at median round 7 (early breakdown). Deals we DO close are thin: seller surplus 12.7% of value (r~8), buyer 11.2% (r~3.2). Walk-away audit: final rejected offers we walked from were mostly correctly bad (mean surplus −0.20 seller / −0.38 buyer) BUT 24/154 (10 seller + 14 buyer) had POSITIVE surplus — real lost deals. Downside model: blanket accept-loosening is dominated by the correctly-rejected 85%; only a positive-surplus-at-terminal accept is safe.
**BARGAINING:** of 436, we-accept 213 (mean realized frac **0.387**, nominal 0.424 — we accept below half), 223 end-unknown (likely opp accepted our offer = our better half, unobservable in turn logs). 82 round-1 accepts. New LLM-named opponents live in pool: llm-1/2/5, "opus 5", "opus 5(4)", Morphling(+Ablation1), Godel, HAWK. Fresh pattern: **extreme-anchor-and-hold** — e.g. opus 5(4) countered our soft 53/47 open with 7/93 and held to r12 where we capitulated (ACC r12). Our r1/r2 accepts vs these run ~0.38-0.47.
**PERSUASION (buyer leg broken vs new field):** buyer buys **100% of low-quality rounds** across ALL opponents and priors in tail — including p=1/3 and p=0.5 games (RESERVE, AK_four) where always-buy is EV≈0-negative; miss-high=0, i.e. buyer degenerates to always-buy. 115 low-buys/1180 rounds, ~$2.65e7 nominal lost across 65 buyer games; buyer payoff = 0.556 of omniscient. Message-decode check: new-field sellers DO send NL low-signals ("I'd pass on this one", "isn't up to...") but revealed-round precision is only 0.59 (26 low vs 18 high, n=44) — decode lever weak/risky; the robust lever is **p-conditioned buy-throttle at p≤0.5** (miss-high risk currently zero-base). Seller leg: sold 853/1318 rounds, 0.646 of max — roughly intact.
**VERDICT:** biggest gap = NEGOTIATION (rating gap −315 to −450 per account; observed 56-75% no-close at median r7 with thin 11-13% surplus on closes). Freshest exploit-shaped findings: (1) neg terminal positive-surplus walk-aways (24/154 in tail) — narrow accept fix candidate; (2) barg extreme-anchor-hold LLM opponents inducing r12 capitulation or sub-0.4 early accepts — needs anchor-resistant counter-path, NOT softer accepts; (3) pers buyer always-buy at low priors — p-scoped throttle. This is scouting; nothing shipped, nothing touched. ~12 tool calls, tails only, no file >tail read.

🔔 FLAGS FOR HUMAN (2026-08-25 23:30 NEW-FIELD BASELINE): **(1) Pers BUYER is degenerate always-buy in the current tail — 100% low-quality buy rate even at p=1/3/p=0.5 (n=115 low-buys/1180 rounds, payoff 0.556 of omniscient).** Note tension with the 08-24 21:20 ⚰️ "don't extend buyer_prior_adj below p=0.6" verdict — that was measured on the OLD pool; vs the new pool the low-p cells are where the bleeding is. Re-run that replay on post-field-change games only before trusting the coffin. **(2) Neg: 24/154 tail games walked away from positive-surplus terminal offers** — a terminal-round positive-surplus accept guard is narrow and models its own downside (the other 130 walks were correct). **(3) Barg: new LLM entrants (opus 5(4) et al.) run extreme-anchor-and-hold (7/93 counters, hold to horizon) and our frozen policy either capitulates at r12 or accepts ~0.38-0.42 early** — old "at-frontier" barg verdicts do not cover this archetype.

## 2026-08-25 ~23:55 — RUN LOG: BARG ANCHOR-HOLD COUNTER (offline validation of scout flag #3; corrected discounted-percentile metric)
**Charter:** validate ONE lever — a scoped "hold firmer vs detected anchor-hold (AH) archetype" — on the CORRECTED metric (discounted-payoff-vs-field percentile, normalized by money_to_divide; reused corrected_barg_scorer.py's field-pool method with opp_payoff/mtd per cell). READ-ONLY; last 300MB tails of games_{champion,gamma,theta,delta}.jsonl (eta barg frozen canary = 0 lines, expected) + last 40k lines of each *_baseline.jsonl. Scratchpad: ah_extract.py + ah_analyze.py + ah_games.jsonl. ~10 tool calls, no live files touched.
**SAMPLE:** 12,025 unique recent barg games (11,015 with server-discounted payoffs joined). AH detector = opponent's opening offer gives us ≤25% of pie AND their total concession ≤7% of pie across ≥1 observed offers → **408 AH games (3.4% of barg; 379 w/ payoff)**. Named AH-rates confirm the scout: opus 5(4) 38%, opus 5(5) 30%, opus 5 23%; but AH is NOT LLM-exclusive (Ra-P/Ra-N 16-18%, katan 15%, crown-agent 18%) — detect behaviorally, not by name.
**CURRENT BEHAVIOR vs AH (the capitulation, quantified):** mean discounted payoff/pie **0.142**, mean field-pctl **0.083** (vs 0.426/0.394 in non-AH — a 4.7x pctl crater). We accept in **198/379 (52%) at median round 12, mean accepted frac 0.154** (the scout's "accepts ~0.38-0.42" was the early-accept minority; the dominant failure is r12 capitulation at ~0.15 → pctl **0.0074**, statistically indistinguishable from a no-deal since pctl(0)=0.000 in every cell). Worst cells: δ0.8/δ0.9 accepts at pctl 0.000-0.001; δ1.0 accepts frac 0.061 at r12 with NO discount excuse.
**HELD-GAMES EVIDENCE (what happens when we don't cave):** among 181 AH games where we held, **opponent caved (accepted OUR standing ~0.53 counter) 130/181 = 71.8%** (mean disc/pie 0.267, pctl 0.231); no-deal 49/181 = 27.1%. Split by horizon: known-hz held n=33 → **cave 33/33 = 100%, median cave round 3**; mr? held n=148 → cave 66% / no-deal 34%. SELECTION CAVEAT (disclosed): held-vs-accepted differ (held are 82% mr?, accepted 84% known-hz; opponents who survive to r8-12 without caving are plausibly more committed than the median-r3 cavers) → the 72-100% empirical cave rate is an UPPER bound for the currently-accepted population; all numbers below therefore swept over q_cave.
**COUNTERFACTUAL (hold our ~0.53 standing counter, cave assumed at final_round+2 capped at mr, no-deal=0 otherwise) — DOWNSIDE MODELED EXPLICITLY:** converting the 198 accepts to holds adds up to ~54 no-deals at q=0.72 (or ALL 198 at q=0) — but because the capitulation payoffs are already pctl 0.0074, the WORST CASE (q=0, every accept becomes a no-deal) costs only −0.0074 pctl/game ≈ **−0.8 family pts**; break-even cave rate = **3.6%**. Sweep: q=0.20 → +0.034 pctl/game = **+4.4 pts**; q=0.35 → **+8.5**; q=0.50 → **+12.5**; q=0.72 (empirical) → **+18.4**; known-hz-only subset at its own q=1.0 → +24.2. This is the mirror-image of the usual close-rate-dominates finding: HERE (and only here) the closes being risked are nearly worthless, so the no-deal conversion penalty that killed tail-toughness (08-21) does not bind.
**VERDICT: GO (net-positive) — scoped anchor-hold counter ≈ +4 to +8 family barg pts conservative (q=0.2-0.35), +12-18 if empirical cave rates transfer, worst-case −0.8** — at/above the +6.5-14 bar of the 08-24 barg ships, with a bounded, provably tiny downside. Shape: detect AH in-policy (opp opening ≤0.25 to us + concession ≤0.07 by ~r3-5, live in state.history), then suppress the late-round accept-capitulation (do NOT accept below a δ-aware floor ≫0.154 vs a detected AH) while keeping our ~0.52-0.53 counter standing. NOT shipped — this changes accepts into rejects (unlike the accept-side-only grind/hz_bank levers), so despite the bounded offline downside it should go as a live A/B on ONE carrier first, exactly like patience_k's status. Scope guard: 3.4% of barg games, detector false-positives self-limiting (a conceding opponent un-flags).

🔔 FLAGS FOR HUMAN (2026-08-25 23:55 BARG ANCHOR-HOLD COUNTER): **(1) GO — the r12-capitulation vs anchor-holders is confirmed net-negative vs holding: our 198 recent capitulations (median r12, frac 0.154) sit at field-pctl 0.0074 ≈ no-deal-equivalent under the corrected discounted metric, while holding wins the opponent's cave 72-100% empirically (100% in known-horizon, n=33) with break-even at just 3.6%.** Central value +4-8 barg pts (up to +18); worst case −0.8. **(2) Ship path: live A/B on one carrier (NOT offline-ship)** — the lever invents rejects, and the cave-rate estimate carries a held-vs-accepted selection confound (held games skew mr?/early-cavers). **(3) Detector spec that worked offline: opp opening gives us ≤0.25 of pie AND opp total concession ≤0.07 of pie — catches opus 5*/llm entrants (23-38% of their games) AND legacy holders (Ra-*, katan); do not gate on opponent name.**

## RUN LOG 2026-08-25 — PERS-BUYER NEW-FIELD RE-TEST (low-p buyer decline gate vs changed field)
**Question:** a new-field scout flagged our pers BUYER as a "degenerate always-buy" (buys ~100% of low-quality offers incl. p=1/3, p=0.5; only ~0.556 of omniscient) and asked whether the 2026-08-24 "do NOT extend buyer gates below p=0.6" verdict is stale now that frontier LLM sellers entered. Re-tested OFFLINE on RECENT games only (tail 150MB x 5 farms).
**Sample:** 2,382 recent buyer-side persuasion games, 44,452 resolved rounds, all 15 (p, r) cells, 137-178 games/cell.
**Finding 1 — the scout's premise is a CONDITIONING ARTIFACT.** In our logs `quality` is only revealed on BOUGHT rounds (verified: 9,817 bought all have quality, 7,944 unbought all lack it) — so "100% of visible lows were bought" is true by construction and says nothing about buy discipline. Actual buy rates in low-p cells: p=1/3 r≤1.25 → 2.9-3.7%; p=1/3 r=2 → 26.5%; p=0.5 r≤1.25 → 22-23%. Overall buyrate 0.577; capture vs EXPECTED-omniscient = 0.706 (not 0.556).
**Finding 2 — the buyer is already SELECTIVE and beats the prior everywhere.** P(high|bought) >> p in every cell: 0.76-0.89 at p=1/3 thin cells, 0.86 at p=0.5 r=1.2/1.25 (break-even ~0.83/0.80), 0.68-0.69 at r=2 cells. New-field sellers STILL selectively push genuinely good units at low p — the 08-24 mechanism is intact, arguably stronger.
**Finding 3 — counterfactual prior-EV decline gate (refuse when p·v+(1−p)·u < price) is NET NEGATIVE on the new field:** −0.422 ± 0.040 price-units per affected game (1,100 affected games, ~10σ), −464 price-units total. Per-cell: the gate destroys realized profit in 5 of 6 firing cells (worst p=1/3 r=2: −1.80/g; p=0.8 r=1.2: −0.92/g; p=0.5 r=1.2/1.25: −0.16/−0.32/g). The ONLY cell where it helps is p=1/3 r=1.2 (+0.060 ± 0.073/g — <1σ, noise; that cell's realized pay is −0.06±0.07/g, i.e. ~breakeven already).
**VERDICT: NO-GO. The Aug-24 verdict is NOT stale — it is re-confirmed on the post-field-change pool, and the proposed gate is far below the +4-12 pers bar (it is strongly negative, not marginally positive).** The buyer leg is not the degenerate leak the scout claimed; do not add low-p decline gates. If anything is ever revisited here, the only defensible micro-scope is p=1/3 r=1.2 alone, and current data says even that is ~0.
🔔 FLAG: none actionable — this closes the re-test. Scout methodology note for future runs: never estimate low-quality buy-rate or omniscient capture from revealed `quality` (purchase-conditioned); use expected-omniscient p·(r−1)·rounds.

### 2026-08-26 00:30 — NEG NO-DEAL HUNT NEW-FIELD (Fable, offline; gamma+theta 300MB game-log tails joined to 5-farm baseline tails; n=4,664 neg games w/ outcome, 2,286 fails)
**Question:** on the changed field, are our neg no-deals concentrated in a detectable, convertible cell — or is the ~56-75% no-close structural? **Answer: ~97% structural, but ONE small, precisely-scoped convertible cell exists: CI-h0 terminal deadlock.**
- **Fail anatomy (2,286 fails):** h1 no_deal 1,317 (58%) / h0 walked_away 479 (21%) / h0 no_deal 341 (15%) / h1 walked_away 148 (6%).
- **h1 no-deals are 0.00% convertible (1/1317):** opponents NEVER placed a profitable offer on the table. Per-cell nd rates track the structural no-ZOPA mix almost exactly (buyer v1.5 nd=0.19 vs ~25% structural; v1.2 nd=0.39 vs ~50%; v1.0 nd=0.67 vs ~75%; v0.8/seller-v1.5 nd=1.00 = correctly-unclosable cells). We already close AT or slightly ABOVE the positive-ZOPA share in every h1 cell — guaranteed_close + akeep(last_round)=0 are doing their job. The scout's "we close only ~25% of tail neg" is mostly no-ZOPA mass, NOT a policy leak. **h1 close-rate is maxed; no +50-80 fix exists there.**
- **Walkaways (627) are dead too:** median walk round 11 (p25=7 — matches the scout's "breakdown r7"), and only 5/627 (0.8%) had any profitable standing offer when the opponent quit. Converting them needs fast EARLY concession = the known-dead blanket-concession family. Confirmed dead.
- **THE convertible cell: h0 no_deal, 61/341 (18%) had a profitable opponent offer we rejected — and 60/61 are COMPLETE-INFO games** (e.g. cost 1.2M, buyer standing 1.305M = +105k for us, we counter 1.47M until the hidden r98-99 cap kills it). All 61 still had a profitable STANDING offer at r>=85 (mean +0.083x our value, median +0.070). Opponent offers in these games flatline for 50+ rounds (72.8/77.6 ping-pong) — they will never cave.
- **Dead-family adjacency (honest flag):** same CI-h0 cell as neg_h0_floor_decay (validated −305, DO NOT re-test). But mechanically distinct: that lever relaxed the fairshare floor MID-GAME (offer-side) and forfeited the 82% of opponents who cave to our high counter. This one is a TERMINAL ACCEPT-side trigger: at r>=96 in h0 (games hard-cap ~r98-99), accept any standing profitable offer. It fires only after the cave-window is exhausted: only 5.8% of h0 closes happen r>=85 (43/745), so r>=96 forfeits ~2-3 rounds of residual cave-time on a ~1-2% slice of closes. Downside bounded ~−2-4 pts worst case; upside 61 conversions/sample from 0 → +0.083x value.
- **VERDICT: GO (small), rough +10-25 neg pts** — `neg_h0_terminal_accept` (h0-only, r>=96, accept-if-profitable; accept-side so replay-validatable, never-a-loss preserved). It is NOT the dead family in disguise (terminal accept ≠ mid-game floor relax), but it lives next door — validate with the same CF discipline that saved the −305. The bigger prize (+50-80) does NOT exist: h1 no-close and walkaways are structurally unconvertible on this field.
🔔 FLAG: add `neg_h0_terminal_accept` (h0, r>=96, accept standing offer iff profitable) to the relaunch-bundle candidate queue as a small accept-side rider; expected +10-25 neg, worst case −2-4. Also: the stale 2026-08-13 note "unknown-horizon already closes, 0% no-deal" is FALSE on the new field (h0 nd now 24-66% by cell) — do not rely on it in future scoping.

## 2026-08-26 ~02:00 — RUN LOG: LLM-PERS A/B + PROMPT INTEL (Fable, read-only)
**Charter:** evaluate the LIVE eta GLEE_LLM_MSG=persuasion arm (haiku-4.5 writes seller message TEXT; numbers deterministic) vs the deterministic-bank control (delta+champion, and eta's own bank-fallback games), then mine winner sellers' messages for prompt improvements. Data: tails of games_{eta,delta,champion}.jsonl (600/300/300MB) joined via game_id to *_baseline.jsonl; scratchpad scan_pers.py / ab.py. Arm ID per game = any seller message ∉ union(messages v1/v2/v3) → LLM-touched (dose was 1.00 in all 341 touched games; delta/champion 0 LLM as expected — clean control).
**A/B RESULT — LLM arm is NEGATIVE, ~3-5σ on volume metrics.** Recent seller-side text games: eta-LLM n=341 vs eta-bank n=1045 vs delta+champ n=1043. Sales/round 0.416±0.023 (LLM) vs 0.542±0.013 (eta-bank, z=−4.8) vs 0.562±0.012 (ctrl). Realized share 0.478±0.019 vs 0.521±0.010 (z=−2.1) vs 0.510±0.010; cell-stratified (p×price) share diff LLM−bank = **−2.7pp ± 2.0 (z=−1.4)**. High-quality conversion collapses: hiConv 0.408 vs 0.575/0.655. Effect direction is uniform across all 8 (p,price) cells.
**ROOT CAUSE — NOT a prompt-quality problem; a WIRING bug that inverts the message.** Per-round table (200MB eta tail): in LLM games **99.8% of HIGH-quality-round messages are anti-buy** ("I'd recommend waiting… the fundamentals don't support buying") vs **0.0%** in bank games (same policy, same params). Chain: (1) policy.py:914 already rewrites via llm_message with the CORRECT signal (det_msg=_BUY_MSG → BUY); (2) strategy.py:~110 bank-swap under pers_msg_fix=1 (set in params_eta.json) keys `action.message == policy._BUY_MSG` — the LLM sentence never equals it → **key="low" → anti-sell bank text clobbers the LLM's positive sentence**; (3) strategy.py:150 then calls llm_message a SECOND time (double cap burn, 2 API calls/message), _sig_of reads the low-bank text → signal=HOLD → haiku faithfully writes "don't buy". Net: **our seller told every buyer not to buy on every round, including highs.** Buyers only half-listen (P(buy|neg msg, high)=0.48) which is why damage is −0.13 sales/rd not total. The LLM's true output never reached a buyer on push rounds — **the "does LLM messaging beat templates" experiment has not actually run yet.**
**WINNER MESSAGE INTEL** (top opponent sellers vs our buyers, by realized seller-share: zoro 1.03, Priori 0.91, Ousen 0.89, new_baby 0.88, Tiberius 0.88, Kairos 0.88; n=20-700 msgs each): (a) tiny message sets — uniq 3-7 templates, short, plain; (b) direct imperative + explicit quality/price claims with parseable keywords ("High quality this round. I recommend it", "Buy this one, it is worth the price"); (c) personal-verification / reputation-staking ("checked it myself", "I'd stake my record on it" — Ousen/Kairos/new_baby); (d) credibility-contrast holds ("Skip this one — I only get paid when you buy, so when I do recommend, it means something" — Kairos); (e) strictly in-domain (unit/round/quality/price — zero market-timing talk). OUR LLM output vs that: finance metaphors ("fundamentals", "entry point", "valuations", "price will drop next week" — fabricates nonexistent price dynamics), no quality claims, no keywords, and **941/1023 sampled messages are "recommend"+negation parser-traps** ("I'd recommend waiting") that naive keyword buyers can misread as a rec → lemon-buy → trust burn.
**PROPOSED (NOT APPLIED — read-only): 1) FIX WIRING FIRST**: single LLM rewrite AFTER the bank-swap (skip strategy's pers bank-swap when llm.message_enabled("persuasion"), and drop the policy.py:912-914 early call OR the strategy.py:150 call — currently both fire); pass the push signal EXPLICITLY into _msg_prompt instead of _sig_of keyword-sniffing. **2)** BUY prompt: require imperative + quality claim + the literal words "recommend"/"buy"/"worth the price"; give 2-3 winner-style exemplars (Priori/Ousen) and ask for light variation. **3)** HOLD prompt: forbid the word "recommend" and any market/price-forecast language; use the Kairos credibility-contrast frame so holds bank trust for future pushes. **4)** Ban finance metaphors globally ("fundamentals/entry point/valuations/market conditions"). **5)** Only genuine LLM upside over v3 bank = opponent-conditioning: feed the buyer's last message + follow history and mirror their wording; otherwise the bank already implements winner style.
**VERDICT: LLM-pers arm as deployed = NET NEGATIVE (−0.13 sales/rd z≈−4.8; share −2.7 to −4.3pp, ~1.4-2.1σ; n=341 treated seller games)** — but this measures the bug, not LLM messaging. Caveat: arm assignment is call-cap/segment-temporal (not randomized), mitigated by cell-stratification + contemporaneous interleaving. Prompt changes alone will NOT lift it while the bank-swap clobber + double-call stand; with the wiring fixed the winner-mined prompt (points 2-5) is a plausible retest.
🔔 FLAGS (LLM-PERS): **(1) ACTIONABLE BUG — strategy.py pers bank-swap keys on `message == policy._BUY_MSG`, which is never true once the LLM (or _HEDGE_MSG!) rewrote the text → every LLM seller message is remapped to the LOW/anti-sell bank line and then re-worded as HOLD; also a double llm_message call burns the ANTHROPIC_MAX_CALLS cap at 2x. Until fixed, eta's pers leg is paying ~−0.13 sales/rd for nothing — consider standing GLEE_LLM_MSG down or shipping the one-line skip.** (2) Side-catch: the same key-by-_BUY_MSG swap would also misroute _HEDGE_MSG pushes to the anti-sell bank in DETERMINISTIC games wherever pers_hedge_lowq is armed — worth a quick audit on carriers running hedge. (3) The real LLM-pers experiment (winner-style prompt, correct wiring) remains UNRUN — the class-gap thesis (glee-class-gap-blindspot) is still untested, not refuted.

## 2026-08-26 — RUN LOG: OPP-COND PERS PREP (Fable, read-only) — buyer REVEALED-COMPLIANCE profiles for per-opponent seller messaging
**Charter:** characterize each frequent BUYER opponent's compliance so the pers seller can condition push-rate/message per-buyer. Data: 300MB tails × 5 games_*.jsonl → 4,634 pers seller-side games (2,326 named-opponent, 163 buyer names; top ~25 buyers profiled at 300-1,700 push-rounds each). Scratchpad buyer_profile.py / buyer_pass2.py / buyer_pass3.py (3 passes: raw compliance → momentum-deconfounded punishment/warmup → quit-threshold + hold-mediated re-entry + momentum-ON template test).
**HEADLINE MECHANISM — buyers are STATE MACHINES, not message readers.** For virtually every buyer: P(buy next push | last push bought)=0.93-1.00 REGARDLESS of whether that buy was a burn (afterBURN 0.85-1.00 vs afterGOOD 0.93-1.00 — per-burn exit hazard only 0-15%), while P(buy next push | last push declined)=0.00-0.05 (absorbing OFF state). Median lemons-bought-before-first-decline = 0-1 → first declines are PRIOR/entry-driven, NOT lie-triggered; "punishment" as classically imagined (tit-for-tat trust loss after a caught lie) basically does not exist in this field. The game per-buyer is: (a) ENTER the ON chain early, (b) manage the small per-burn exit hazard, (c) never waste pushes on an OFF buyer.
**TEXT-BLINDNESS (the LLM-upside question): CONFIRMED text-blind conditional on state.** Momentum-ON conversion is 0.90-1.00 for EVERY template incl. the weak "Solid pick" hedge line; binary-game "yes" compliance ≈ text-game template compliance per buyer (e.g. Test 1: 0.75 vs 0.75). Pass-1's apparent template gaps (king 0.84 vs 0.35, Morphling 0.63 vs 0.26) were pure selection — the hedge line is sent on lows (lowq% 0.40-0.57 vs 0.14-0.38) and to OFF-state buyers. ⇒ LLM free-prose messaging upside is CAPPED: the operative channel is the push/hold binary + buyer's own trust state. Residual text edge (unproven, tiny n): entry-round credibility lines for skeptics (AK_test reput-line 13/13 buys; EV-arithmetic intel from 08-23 entry) + avoiding parser-trap wording that causes accidental OFF flips (the LLM-arm bug's "recommend waiting" traps).
**4 BUYER TYPES + optimal posture:**
- **T1 LOCKED-ON FOLLOWERS** (burn-hazard ≤3%, baseline P(buy|push) 0.79-0.91): expert 0.87, king 0.87, Test 4 0.91, nemo 0.86, velocity 0.84, A1 0.83, crown-agent 0.82, sudsho 0.80, Optimal 0.79, new_baby 0.75 (buys holds 0.51!). POSTURE: push ~100% of rounds incl. all lemons (chain doesn't break); any plain push line; minimize holds (they obey holds at 0.00-0.09 = pure forfeited sales, except new_baby/Test 4 who buy through holds).
- **T2 FRAGILE TRUSTERS** (entry 0.86-0.98 r1-3, burn-hazard 5-13%, absorbing exit): Test 1/2/3, agent, opus 5(+2/4/5), Morphling, Equilibrist. POSTURE: push all highs; METER lemons early (lemon-push is +EV iff price > hazard × E[remaining ON revenue] → cap lemons before ~r8, free-push last 5-6 rounds); one decline = chain dead (re-entry 0-5%) → after decline go pure-honest/stop wasting pushes.
- **T3 HARD SKEPTICS** (entry-limited, baseline 0.32-0.58, afterDECLINE 0.00, no re-entry): top glee 0.43, HAWK 0.32, Kairos 0.52, Priori 0.54, Ousen 0.52, grok 4.6 0.55, Clod 0.58, GoldenSon. Their afterGOOD is still 0.94-1.00 → hard to start, easy to keep. POSTURE: first ~3 rounds push ONLY highs (Ousen r1-3 0.94 → 0.43 later; the entry window is everything), keep purity until chain established, meter lemons late; a push at an OFF skeptic converts 0.00 — total waste.
- **T4 NOISY/PRIOR BUYERS** (non-absorbing, buy holds, re-enter): Radium (hold 0.41, re-entry 0.17-0.18), Ra-N (hold 0.29, consec re-entry 0.16), AK_test (hold 0.28), Utk (viaHOLD re-entry 0.11, INVERSE warmup 0.48→0.64), TMachine (viaHOLD 0.75, n=4). POSTURE: push relentlessly, ignore declines (noise not grudge), text irrelevant.
- **Hold-mediated RE-ENTRY** (the F1 liar-gate mechanism) works only on a minority: A1 0.44, agent 0.21, Radium 0.17, Utk 0.11, TMachine 0.75(n=4) — vs consec-push 0.00-0.05. For everyone else declined = dead; don't spend rounds trying.
**VERDICT:** per-opponent conditioning is real but it's a PUSH-POLICY dial, not a prose dial: (1) T1/T4 → raise push-rate to ~1.0 (we currently hold ~20-30% of rounds vs these = pure lost sales; largest single upside), (2) T2 → lemon-metering by remaining-rounds × hazard, (3) T3 → highs-only entry window then blend. LLM messaging per se: capped, entry-window credibility lines are the only untested residual.
🔔 FLAGS (OPP-COND PERS): **(1) ACTIONABLE — vs T1 followers (expert/king/Test 4/nemo/velocity/A1/crown-agent/sudsho, jointly ~400 games in tail window) every hold is a forfeited sale (they obey holds ~0.00-0.09 and don't punish burns, hazard ≤3%): an opponent-conditioned push-rate override (push all rounds vs T1 names) is the cleanest quantified pers lever from this study.** (2) Do NOT push at OFF-state buyers (afterDECLINE ≈ 0.00 for 20/25 profiled) — a state-aware "stop pushing after decline unless T4/re-enterable" rule saves credibility-free wasted rounds and pairs with F1. (3) LLM-pers ceiling: buyers are text-blind given state → fix the eta wiring bug but expect prose gains only in T3 entry rounds; prioritize the push-policy dials above prompt work.

## 2026-08-26 — RUN LOG: NEG ZOPA-EXTRACTION CRACK (Fable, read-only)
**Charter:** quantify the neg extraction gap vs top agents (Clod, grok 4.6, gill bates, Mythos01, Test 4, opus 5(+variants)) and test whether it's opponent-conditioned (fold-vs-walk). Data: 98,904 negotiation baseline records (5 carriers) joined by game_id to ripgrep-filtered games_*.jsonl decision logs (3,481 lines / 469 unique H2H games vs named tops, 434 matched w/ offer history). Confirmed games_*.jsonl only logs OUR decisions (current_player constant per game_id) — opponent offers live in the embedded history[] array, so opponent trajectories vs tops are already in our own logs. Scripts: scratchpad analyze_baseline.py / analyze_traj.py / analyze_early.py. Full writeup: scratchpad study_neg_crack_0826.md.
**Q1 GAP SIZE:** our share-on-deals vs named non-top opponents = 52.43% (n=23,968 deals) vs vs named TOP agents = 47.05% (n=792 deals) — **gap ≈ 5.4 share-points**, close-rate flat at ~50% both ways (tops don't close less, they extract more per deal). Per-agent: Clod 39.1%, grok 4.6 43.3%, opus5(2) 45.7%, opus5 48.0%, Test 4 51.5% (n=107-624 each, noisy but consistent direction).
**Q2/Q3 KEY FINDING (robust, n=434, holds within every opponent-type stratum):** OUR OWN cumulative concession magnitude predicts failure, not the opponent's. us=fold (we conceded >15% of range over the game): close rate 13.4%, share-on-deal 32.65% (n=112, 15 deals). us=walk/hold (we conceded <5%): close rate 70.1%, share-on-deal 53.43% (n=117, 82 deals). Same direction in every opp-class × our-class cell (e.g. opp=walk: us=walk 84.2%close/48.7%share vs us=fold 16%close/24.75%share). **Independently corroborates the same-day H2H study (study_h2h_0826.md): we NEVER get our own offer accepted in a decided top-agent H2H game — pure reactive/accept-only negotiator.** Interpretation: gradual multi-round self-concession vs top agents is NOT reciprocated (fold-harvest asymmetry, per glee-opponent-formula-cracked.md) — they let it run to no-deal instead of meeting us partway, so grinding down converts winnable/breakeven games into no-deals. Counterfactual ceiling if fold-pattern games played as hold-pattern: +56.7pp close, +20.8pp share — but this is an UPPER bound (game-difficulty confound not fully separable: some folds happen because the game is genuinely hard). No-deal downside of holding harder does NOT reproduce here (holding correlates with MORE closes, not fewer) — different failure mode than the earlier indiscriminate-squeeze tests.
**Q4 MID-GAME SIGNAL:** restricted to games w/ ≥3 opponent offers (n=85, only ~20% of top-opp games even qualify — rest are short h1/instant). Using ONLY opponent's first 3 offers (~round 5-6): fold-prone (>10% range movement) → 69.4% close, 66.57% share (n=49, 34 deals, already good for us). walk-prone (<3% movement) → 100%* close (likely selection artifact), 46.01% share (n=36, 36 deals — WE are caving here). Early/full-game classification agreement only 75% (64/85) — moderately stable, not tight. Directionally the signal exists; thresholds not yet tuned at this n.
**VERDICT / GO-NO-GO: GO on the robust SELF-conditioned lever** — cap our own concession budget (~10-15% of range); once exceeded without opponent reciprocation, STOP conceding rather than grinding further. Low risk (doesn't require an opponent classifier at all, direction consistent at n=100+/stratum, associated with MORE closes not fewer). **NO-GO (yet) on hard-coded opponent fold/walk thresholds** from Q4 — right direction, but n=85 is too thin to pick exact cutoffs; revisit as baseline.record accumulates more opponent+game_id-tagged top-agent games (capturing since 2026-08-22).
🔔 FLAGS (NEG ZOPA-EXTRACTION CRACK): **(1) ACTIONABLE — self-conditioned concession-budget cap (stop conceding past ~10-15% of range without reciprocation) is the cleanest, lowest-risk buildable neg lever from this study; ~+21 share-point ceiling (likely confounded downward) on the ~112/434 games where we currently over-concede vs top agents.** (2) Confirms (independently, same day as study_h2h_0826.md) that we are a reactive/accept-only negotiator — never get our own offer accepted vs top agents — reinforcing that the fix is on the CONCESSION/HOLD side, not the offer/proposal side. (3) Opponent-conditioned fold/walk detection (Q4) is directionally real but underpowered (n=85) — do not hard-code thresholds yet; a good candidate for re-check once more top-agent games accumulate.

- close-rate 0.471 vs 0.416 → raw +0.055 (2-prop z≈1.8, p≈0.07); BUT champion same-clock close also rose +0.047 → **close DiD ≈ +0.008 ≈ zero** (the first read's "+0.04 close DiD, mechanism confirmed" does NOT survive the larger sample).

**2) TUNE — the cap NEVER FIRES.** Reconstructed offer ladders for 243/367 post neg games (124 excluded = no own counter-offer, mr1/instant cells where the cap is moot by construction). Unreciprocated-concession histogram: 186 games ≈0%, 42 games 2–6%, **0 games in 6–14%**, 15 games 14–16% (= reciprocity-RELEASED, cap legitimately lifted). Our policy's unreciprocated concessions max out ~6%, so the 0.12 wall is **structurally inert — a no-op on offers**. - frac counterfactual (directional, offer-side, opponent-response CF — flag: assumes accept logic unchanged): **f=0.06 binds ~0 extra games** (only the released 15, which reciprocity re-opens anyway); **f=0.03 binds the 4–6% bin (49 games, 20% weight) with est. share_all delta +0.000 opt AND pes, 0 deals lost** — because 13/17 deals in concession games closed by US ACCEPTING THEIR offer (accept-side), not by opponents crossing our ladder (they_crossed = 2). Holding the ladder firmer extracts nothing when closes don't happen on our ladder.

Do NOT promote to carriers before the Aug-28 bank — there is nothing to carry, only config-surface risk. Tightening to 0.06/0.03 is a no-op-to-nothing; do not spend another read on it. The inertness is structural, not underpowered — more data will not change this. Consistent with the standing conclusion that neg value lives on the ACCEPT side (already at frontier).

## 2026-08-26 — RUN LOG: OPP-COND PERS PUSH-RATE (offline, read-only; seller-seat pers, last ~350MB x 5 carrier logs)

**Data:** 5,780 seller-seat persuasion games / 108.7k decided rounds (all 5 carriers, recent window). Push/hold classified from history seller_message (binary yes/no + text hold-phrase match). ~51% of seller-seat records still have opponent=null (games from before/without the opponent-logging fix) — name analysis covers the named half.

**1) Forfeited sales vs NAMED followers (test 4/nemo/expert/king/velocity/a1/crown-agent/sudsho; 422 games, 8.0k rounds):**
- P(buy|push)=81.4% (±1.7pp), and critically **P(buy|push, LOW quality)=79.9%** (n=1,709) — followers buy lemons at essentially the good-product rate. P(buy|hold)=1.7%.
- We currently HOLD 24.2% of rounds vs them (**4.67 holds/game, 1,999/2,001 on low quality**) → **~3.7 forfeited sales/game**, ~1,570 sales across the window. Sale-rate 0.612→0.825 of max (+21.3pp of max seller payoff) if pushed 100%.

**2) Credibility hazard ≈ nil for followers, VERIFIED:** next-push buy after a bought LEMON = 95.9% (n=1,084) vs 99.6% after a bought good — **hazard 3.6pp** (prior study's ≤3% confirmed at ~4pp). Contrast: UNGATED buyers hazard = 24.3pp — punishment exists in the field, just not in this type.

**3) Detection — behavioral gate BEATS names:** "first 2 pushes both bought" (readable from state.history by ~round 3-4, no classifier):
- Gates in **71.7% of seller-seat games** (4,146). Gated set: post-gate P(buy|push)=86.5%, **P(buy|push,low)=85.3%** (n=19.2k — direct evidence lemon-pushing already converts on this population), hazard only 4.6pp, per-game precision (later P(buy|push)≥0.75) = **82.2%**.
- Gated set still holds 13.5% of post-gate rounds (2.13 holds/game) → converting them = **+10.8pp of max seller payoff on 72% of seller-seat pers games** (sale-rate 0.757→0.865). Named-follower subset post-gate: +13.7pp.
- Ungated set P(buy|push)=11.3% — the gate cleanly separates the punisher/ignorer field.

**4) Downside model:** holds earn ~nothing today (P(buy|hold)=1.7-6.9%), so the option cost of pushing is ~zero; the only risk is the ~18% gated false-positives where lemon pushes could dent later good-round sales (bounded by the 4.6pp aggregate hazard). Mitigation: revoke override after 2 consecutive push-declines (true followers decline <14% per round, 2-in-a-row ≈ <2%).

**VERDICT: GO.** Opponent-conditioned push-100% is a near-pure sales gain, buildable decision-side in the seller push/hold branch (no 6-label classifier): gate = (≥2 pushes bought, no 2 consecutive push-declines since) → always recommend, incl. low quality; optional name allowlist as instant-on for the 8 dossier followers. Payoff math: ≈ +10.8pp-of-max on 72% of seller-seat games ≈ +3.9pp of max across all pers games (buyer seat untouched). Rough points: **+20-50 pers** (pers ~2180, cap ~2450 — headroom exists; compare liar-gate +2.5pct promotion). Interacts with pers_frec_target/lateramp: the override should BYPASS (not multiply) the unconditional push-rate machinery inside gated games.

**🔔 FLAGS:**
- 🔔 ACTIONABLE: build `pers_follower_push100` (behavior-gated, decision-side, ~15 LOC in seller message branch) + 2-decline revocation; ship via the consolidated-carrier arm/relaunch, NOT a mid-drain hot edit.
- 🔔 The gate must scope to SELLER seat + persuasion only; do not let it touch the buyer-seat prior-anchored logic.
- 🔔 "test4" (no space, 6 games) is a DIFFERENT agent from "test 4" (P(buy|push) 22.9% vs 90.3%) — dossier name list must use exact "test 4".
- 🔔 ~51% opponent=null in seller-seat records means name-gating alone covers half the traffic — another reason the behavioral gate is primary.

**Verdict: EARLY-POSITIVE on mechanism / TOO-THIN on outcome.** Recommend: hold on delta and re-read at ~24h post-cutoff (n≈1.5-2k games/arm gives power for the projected lift) BEFORE promoting to gamma. ### 2026-08-26 10:05 — neg_h0_term_round threshold-placement audit (untried: is the shipped h0-terminal-accept's r>=96 trigger leaving 70-95-window conversions on the table, or preempting r97-99 caves?) — Fable, offline read-only; 300MB games_*.jsonl tails x 5 farms streamed in place (no copies) + full *_baseline.jsonl neg joins → 1,131 CI-h0 neg games (1,090 with outcome), 208 reaching r>=70; scratchpad h0term_window.py. **RESULT: r=96 is VALIDATED AT THE OPTIMUM — both directions are decisively negative; do NOT tune neg_h0_term_round.** (1) LOWER is a trap: paired within-game CF(accept first profitable at r>=85) vs CF(r>=96) = **−0.177 ± 0.016 ZOPA-share (n=208, 82 games differ)**; whole ladder T∈{70,75,80,85,90} sits at mean CF zshare 0.32-0.34 vs **T=96 = 0.498** — despite the "opponents flatline" prior, the 85-95 window's continuation value is real (actual HOLD zshare 0.466 vs accept-at-85 0.320; last-offer drift to r96 only +0.011, so the value is late floor-crossing improvements/caves invisible in our own turn log because the opponent's final accept of OUR counter is never logged — cave detection must go through baseline payoffs, a method note for future tail studies). (2) HIGHER is also a trap: games where we REJECTED a profitable offer at r>=96 (n=29, 27 of them delta = the no-lever control) went **28/29 no-deal**, actual 0.032 vs standing-offer 0.260 → holding past 96 = −0.228/game; by r96 the cave has either happened or never will. (3) NO missed-fire risk below the trigger: **zero** payoff=0 games ended at maxr 80-95 (all CI-h0 no-deals run to 96+; no hidden caps under 96, walkaways in this cell ≈ none), and all 104 observed lever fires (accepts at >=96, 4 treated farms) closed at exactly the standing offer (mean zshare 0.261) with 0 harm cases. Side quantification: delta (kept as the intentional pre-bank control, no lever) is forfeiting ~0.23 ZOPA-share/game on its ~27-per-300MB-window r>=96 reject games — re-confirms adding neg_h0_terminal_accept=1 to delta at its first post-bank relaunch. — verdict: dead (no param change — threshold confirmed optimal in both directions; guard result, saves a tempting mis-tune).

(2) **But refusals of SELLER PUSHES are systematically −EV: per-(seller,cell) static-honesty scoring (π̂ from the SAME seller+cell's bought pushes, n≥10; field sellers are static schedules so π(push) is history-invariant) over 5,691 named refusals = +1,449 price-units forgone where ev>0 vs only −111 correctly refused (13:1 mis-refusal ratio) = 3.5% of window buyer payoff (41,442 units); null-opponent refusal mass is the same size again (2,681 vs 2,635 in be≤0.5 cells) → ~5-7% total.** Worst mis-refused sellers all-+EV: GoldenSon (π .65-.72), Utk (1.00 @ratio1.25!), Test 3 (.64-.76), harry-agent (.48-.75), frodo (.55-.93). (3) **Mechanism localized, 2 parts: (a) ABSORBING REFUSAL STREAKS — we are the same state-machine we farm in field buyers: follow 0.93-0.96 at streak=0 collapses to 0.02-0.39 once refusing starts, while π_bought DURING streaks stays 0.75-0.89 (be≤0.5 bands) — the OFF state is miscalibrated vs a field whose push honesty doesn't drop; (b) COLD-START UNDER-FOLLOW at (p0.33, ratio2): follow 0.475 at burns=0 (n=2,277) vs named static π .73-.76 and be=0.50 → CF(follow pushes while burns≤1, π=0.70 conservative) = +527 units/window in that cell pair alone.** (4) Correctly-refused stays correct: ratio-1.25 low-p (be=0.8, π .64-.87 < be in p≤0.5) and all HOLD rounds (accounting identity puts π(hold) ≈ 0.03-0.12 in binary cells) — do NOT buy holds; the lever must scope to PUSH rounds in be≤0.5 cells only. **VERDICT: GO-candidate `buyer_push_floor` (2-part: ratio-2 p≤0.5 cold-start follow-pushes-until-2-burns + refusal-streak probe/π-floor ~0.6 in be≤0.5 cells), est +15-35 pers pts on the weak buyer leg; needs exact-replay CF then live A/B.** Honest adjacency: the dead buyer_prior_adj_pmin<0.6 (08-24 21:20) was the clf-formula prior-anchoring direction (weaken the push signal at low p) — this is the OPPOSITE direction (credit the push signal; that flag's own P(high|pushed)=0.64 at p=1/3 measurement supports it); mechanically distinct from the dead lever but validate with the same discipline. — verdict: promising / needs-replay-CF + live test.
